DRA incorporates adaptive dual attention and diversity-promoting multi-head attention to fuse multimodal information, from three modalities, based on surgical context, enhancing assessment accuracy and reliability. Validated on the JIGSAWS benchmark, RAH-skill, and RARP-skill datasets, our approach outperforms recent baselines with SCC improvements of 0.02 in LOSO, 0.04 in LOUO across JIGSAWS tasks, and 0.0538 and 0.0493 gains on RAH-skill and RARP-skill, respectively.

</details>


### [337] [PEPA: a Persistently Autonomous Embodied Agent with Personalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00117)
*Kaige Liu,Yang Li,Lijun Zhu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PEPA三层认知架构，利用人格特质实现机器人持久自主性，无需外部预设任务，在真实办公环境中验证了五种人格原型的稳定行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体依赖外部预设目标，限制了在动态非结构化环境中的长期部署能力。生物体通过内部生成目标和自我维持行为组织实现持久自主性，人格特质可作为实现类似自主性的内在组织原则

Method: 提出PEPA三层认知架构：Sys3自主合成人格对齐目标并通过情景记忆和每日自省进行优化；Sys2进行深思熟虑推理将目标转化为可执行行动计划；Sys1负责传感器运动交互，执行动作并记录经验。在四足机器人上部署验证

Result: 在多楼层办公楼环境中成功部署，机器人能够自主仲裁用户请求和人格驱动动机，自主导航电梯和探索环境。对五种不同人格原型的定量分析展示了稳定、特质对齐的行为模式

Conclusion: 人格驱动的认知架构能够实现持续自主操作，具备持久具身系统的特征，为在动态非结构化环境中长期部署自主机器人提供了可行方案

Abstract: Living organisms exhibit persistent autonomy through internally generated goals and self-sustaining behavioral organization, yet current embodied agents remain driven by externally scripted objectives. This dependence on predefined task specifications limits their capacity for long-term deployment in dynamic, unstructured environments where continuous human intervention is impractical. We propose that personality traits provide an intrinsic organizational principle for achieving persistent autonomy. Analogous to genotypic biases shaping biological behavioral tendencies, personalities enable agents to autonomously generate goals and sustain behavioral evolution without external supervision. To realize this, we develop PEPA, a three-layer cognitive architecture that operates through three interacting systems: Sys3 autonomously synthesizes personality-aligned goals and refines them via episodic memory and daily self-reflection; Sys2 performs deliberative reasoning to translate goals into executable action plans; Sys1 grounds the agent in sensorimotor interaction, executing actions and recording experiences. We validate the framework through real-world deployment on a quadruped robot in a multi-floor office building. Operating without reliance on fixed task specifications, the robot autonomously arbitrates between user requests and personality-driven motivations, navigating elevators and exploring environments accordingly. Quantitative analysis across five distinct personality prototypes demonstrates stable, trait-aligned behaviors. The results confirm that personality-driven cognitive architectures enable sustained autonomous operation characteristic of persistent embodied systems. Code and demo videos are available at https://sites.google.com/view/pepa-persistent/.

</details>


### [338] [Multiview Progress Prediction of Robot Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00151)
*Elena Zoppellari,Federico Becattini,Marco Fiorucci,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多视角架构用于机器人操作任务中的动作进度预测，解决单视角因自遮挡导致的感知限制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要理解动作进度以安全有效地与人类协作，但现有研究常忽视动作进度建模，且单视角相机因自遮挡问题难以准确感知机器人自身动作

Method: 提出多视角架构用于机器人操作任务中的动作进度预测，通过多视角信息克服单视角的自遮挡限制

Result: 在Mobile ALOHA平台上进行实验，验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 多视角架构能有效提升机器人动作进度预测能力，解决单视角感知限制问题，为机器人安全协作提供技术支持

Abstract: For robots to operate effectively and safely alongside humans, they must be able to understand the progress of ongoing actions. This ability, known as action progress prediction, is critical for tasks ranging from timely assistance to autonomous decision-making. However, modeling action progression in robotics has often been overlooked. Moreover, a single camera may be insufficient for understanding robot's ego-actions, as self-occlusion can significantly hinder perception and model performance. In this paper, we propose a multi-view architecture for action progress prediction in robot manipulation tasks. Experiments on Mobile ALOHA demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [339] [Trust in Autonomous Human--Robot Collaboration: Effects of Responsive Interaction Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00154)
*Shauna Heron,Meng Cheng Lau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究比较了两种人机交互策略对信任形成的影响：响应式策略（机器人主动适应）与中性反应式策略（仅提供直接任务响应），发现响应式策略在可行通信条件下能显著提升信任，但通信质量下降时优势减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任在人机协作中至关重要，但很少在完全自主交互的约束下研究其形成机制。本研究旨在探索在无Wizard-of-Oz控制或脚本修复的真实自主交互中，交互策略如何影响信任形成。

Method: 采用试点研究设计，参与者与移动机器人完成多阶段协作任务。机器人完全自主管理语音对话、情感推断和任务进展。比较两种交互策略：响应式策略（基于推断的交互状态主动调整对话和协助）与中性反应式策略（仅在提示时提供直接任务相关响应）。

Result: 在可行通信条件下，响应式交互与显著更高的交互后信任相关，尽管整体任务准确性无可靠差异。敏感性分析显示：信任的情感和体验成分比可靠性评估判断对通信故障更敏感；随着语言介导交互质量下降，响应式策略的信任优势减弱，评分难以清晰解释为协作能力的校准评估。

Conclusion: 自主人机交互中的信任源于过程级交互动态，并受通信可行性的约束。研究强调了在设计交互式机器人系统时，在真实自主条件下评估信任的重要性。

Abstract: Trust plays a central role in human--robot collaboration, yet its formation is rarely examined under the constraints of fully autonomous interaction. This pilot study investigated how interaction policy influences trust during in-person collaboration with a social robot operating without Wizard-of-Oz control or scripted repair. Participants completed a multi-stage collaborative task with a mobile robot that autonomously managed spoken-language dialogue, affect inference, and task progression. Two interaction policies were compared: a responsive policy, in which the robot proactively adapted its dialogue and assistance based on inferred interaction state, and a neutral, reactive policy, in which the robot provided only direct, task-relevant responses when prompted. Responsive interaction was associated with significantly higher post-interaction trust under viable communication conditions, despite no reliable differences in overall task accuracy. Sensitivity analyses indicated that affective and experiential components of trust were more sensitive to communication breakdown than evaluative judgments of reliability, and that as language-mediated interaction degraded, the trust advantage associated with responsiveness attenuated and ratings became less clearly interpretable as calibrated evaluations of collaborative competence. These findings suggest that trust in autonomous human--robot interaction emerges from process-level interaction dynamics and operates within constraints imposed by communication viability, highlighting the importance of evaluating trust under real autonomy conditions when designing interactive robotic systems.

</details>


### [340] [EgoMoD: Predicting Global Maps of Dynamics from Local Egocentric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00167)
*Iacopo Catalano,David Morilla-Cabello,Jorge Pena-Queralta,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoMoD：首个从机器人短时第一人称视频预测未来动态地图的方法，通过局部动态线索推断全局运动趋势，实现有限观测下的长期动态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中高效导航需要预测超出机器人即时感知范围的运动模式，实现前瞻性而非纯反应式规划。传统动态地图构建需要长时间全局环境观测，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出EgoMoD方法，通过视频和姿态条件架构，从短时第一人称视频片段学习预测未来动态地图。使用外部观测计算的动态地图作为特权监督，训练模型从局部动态线索推断环境范围的运动趋势。

Result: 在大型模拟环境中，EgoMoD在有限观测条件下准确预测未来动态地图；真实图像评估显示其零样本迁移到真实系统的能力。

Conclusion: EgoMoD首次实现了从局部第一人称观测预测全局动态地图，为机器人导航提供了超越视野限制的动态预测能力，具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Efficient navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating how motion patterns evolve beyond the robot's immediate perceptual range, enabling preemptive rather than purely reactive planning in crowded scenes. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) offer a structured representation of motion tendencies in space useful for long-term global planning, but constructing them traditionally requires global environment observations over extended periods of time. We introduce EgoMoD, the first approach that learns to predict future MoDs directly from short egocentric video clips collected during robot operation. Our method learns to infer environment-wide motion tendencies from local dynamic cues using a video- and pose-conditioned architecture trained with MoDs computed from external observations as privileged supervision, allowing local observations to serve as predictive signals of global motion structure. Thanks to this, we offer the capacity to forecast future motion dynamics over the whole environment rather than merely extend past patterns in the robot's field of view. Experiments in large simulated environments show that EgoMoD accurately predicts future MoDs under limited observability, while evaluation with real images showcases its zero-shot transferability to real systems.

</details>


### [341] [Embedding Morphology into Transformers for Cross-Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00182)
*Kei Suzuki,Jing Liu,Ye Wang,Chiori Hori,Matthew Brand,Diego Romeres,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过运动学令牌、拓扑感知注意力偏置和关节属性条件化三种机制注入形态信息的机器人策略学习方法，提高了跨机器人策略学习的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器人策略学习（训练单一策略在多种机器人形态上表现良好）是机器人学习中的核心挑战。现有的Transformer策略（如视觉-语言-动作模型）通常是形态无关的，需要从观察中推断运动学结构，这会降低跨形态的鲁棒性，甚至限制单一形态内的性能。

Method: 提出了形态感知的Transformer策略，通过三种机制注入形态信息：1）运动学令牌：将动作按关节分解并通过每关节时间分块压缩时间维度；2）拓扑感知注意力偏置：将运动学拓扑编码为自注意力中的归纳偏置，鼓励沿运动学边的信息传递；3）关节属性条件化：用每关节描述符增强拓扑，捕捉连接性之外的语义信息。

Result: 在多种机器人形态上的实验表明，这种结构化集成方法相比基础的pi0.5 VLA基线模型，性能得到一致提升，表明在单一形态内和跨形态的鲁棒性都有改善。

Conclusion: 通过将形态信息结构化地集成到Transformer策略中，可以有效提高跨机器人策略学习的性能，增强策略在不同机器人形态上的适应能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cross-robot policy learning -- training a single policy to perform well across multiple embodiments -- remains a central challenge in robot learning. Transformer-based policies, such as vision-language-action (VLA) models, are typically embodiment-agnostic and must infer kinematic structure purely from observations, which can reduce robustness across embodiments and even limit performance within a single embodiment. We propose an embodiment-aware transformer policy that injects morphology via three mechanisms: (1) kinematic tokens that factorize actions across joints and compress time through per-joint temporal chunking; (2) a topology-aware attention bias that encodes kinematic topology as an inductive bias in self-attention, encouraging message passing along kinematic edges; and (3) joint-attribute conditioning that augments topology with per-joint descriptors to capture semantics beyond connectivity. Across a range of embodiments, this structured integration consistently improves performance over a vanilla pi0.5 VLA baseline, indicating improved robustness both within an embodiment and across embodiments.

</details>


### [342] [Modeling PWM-Time-SOC Interaction in a Simulated Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00319)
*Vidyut Pradeep,Shirantha Welikala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个结合物理和数据驱动的模型，用于预测模拟4轮Arduino机器人的电池荷电状态(SOC)随时间与PWM占空比的变化关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人需要准确预测电池荷电状态，以便在不耗尽所有可用电源的情况下规划运动

Method: 建立包含电机电气特性（电阻、电感、反电动势、转矩常数）和机械动力学（质量、阻力、滚动阻力、车轮半径）的前向运动仿真，生成1-100% PWM值的SOC时间序列数据，应用稀疏识别非线性动力学(SINDy)结合最小二乘回归构建统一的非线性模型SOC(t, p)

Result: 开发了一个能够预测SOC随时间与PWM占空比变化的统一非线性模型，该框架支持能量感知规划，并可扩展到包含任意初始SOC水平和环境相关参数

Conclusion: 该物理与数据驱动模型为类似机器人提供了能量感知规划框架，并可扩展用于实际部署，通过纳入任意初始SOC水平和环境相关参数

Abstract: Accurate prediction of battery state of charge is needed for autonomous robots to plan movements without using up all available power. This work develops a physics and data-informed model from a simulation that predicts SOC depletion as a function of time and PWM duty cycle for a simulated 4-wheel Arduino robot. A forward-motion simulation incorporating motor electrical characteristics (resistance, inductance, back-EMF, torque constant) and mechanical dynamics (mass, drag, rolling resistance, wheel radius) was used to generate SOC time-series data across PWM values from 1-100%. Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy), combined with least-squares regression, was applied to construct a unified nonlinear model that captures SOC(t, p). The framework allows for energy-aware planning for similar robots and can be extended to incorporate arbitrary initial SOC levels and environment-dependent parameters for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [343] [Geometric Look-Angle Shaping Strategy for Enclosed Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00325)
*Amit Shivam,Manuel C. R. M. Fernandes,Sergio Vinha,Fernando A. C. C. Fontes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLASS是一种用于无人机封闭区域检测的几何视角整形策略，通过有界的几何一致性视角整形构建飞行器制导指令，确保制导动力学全局存在并避免传统方法的远场限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统制导方法在封闭区域检测中存在远场限制和奇异性问题，需要一种能够确保全局存在性、避免奇异性并满足最大转弯率约束的几何制导策略。

Method: 在极坐标几何框架中嵌入平滑的双曲正切型整形函数，构建几何一致的视角整形策略；通过Lyapunov稳定性分析建立渐近收敛性，并显式考虑曲率可行性条件和最大转弯率约束。

Result: 理论分析表明GLASS能够渐近收敛到预设的检测保持距离，具有明确的稳定时间特性；六自由度四旋翼高保真仿真验证了该方法在代表性封闭检测场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: GLASS为自主封闭检测任务提供了一个实用可行的制导框架，能够避免奇异性、满足操作约束，并在复杂几何环境中保持稳定的检测性能。

Abstract: This paper introduces inspection through GLASS, a Geometric Look-Angle Shaping Strategy for enclosed regions using unmanned aerial vehicles. In doing so, the vehicles guidance command is constructed through a bounded, geometry-consistent shaping of the look angle relative to a desired standoff path. By embedding a smooth, hyperbolic-tangent-type shaping function within a polar geometric framework, GLASS ensures global existence of the guidance dynamics. It avoids the far-field limitations inherent to conventional formulations. Lyapunov stability analysis establishes asymptotic convergence to a prescribed inspection standoff under explicit curvature feasibility conditions, along with analytical settling-time characteristics. The proposed strategy incorporates maximum turn-rate constraints without inducing singularities throughout the workspace. High-fidelity six-degree-of-freedom quadrotor simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of GLASS in representative enclosed inspection scenarios, highlighting a practically viable guidance framework for autonomous enclosed inspection missions.

</details>


### [344] [Layered Safety: Enhancing Autonomous Collision Avoidance via Multistage CBF Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00338)
*Erina Yamaguchi,Ryan M. Bena,Gilbert Bahati,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端的分层安全过滤框架，利用局部感知数据实现动态避障，通过预测安全过滤器和实时CBF安全过滤器两阶段保障系统安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单阶段安全过滤器在动态避障应用中存在局限性，需要更鲁棒和可靠的分层安全过滤框架来处理各种应用场景下的碰撞避免问题。

Method: 基于机器人中心点云构建占据地图，合成泊松安全函数作为控制屏障函数；采用两阶段安全过滤：预测安全过滤器计算最优安全轨迹，实时CBF安全过滤器精炼瞬时速度命令。

Result: 通过详细的帕累托分析验证了多阶段架构相比传统单阶段安全过滤器的优越性和鲁棒性，并在多种腿式机器人平台上展示了该避障方法在真实动态场景中的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 提出的分层安全过滤框架能够为完整系统提供形式化安全保证，在多种真实动态场景中表现出优异的避障性能，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a general end-to-end framework for constructing robust and reliable layered safety filters that can be leveraged to perform dynamic collision avoidance over a broad range of applications using only local perception data. Given a robot-centric point cloud, we begin by constructing an occupancy map which is used to synthesize a Poisson safety function (PSF). The resultant PSF is employed as a control barrier function (CBF) within two distinct safety filtering stages. In the first stage, we propose a predictive safety filter to compute optimal safe trajectories based on nominal potentially-unsafe commands. The resultant short-term plans are constrained to satisfy the CBF condition along a finite prediction horizon. In the second stage, instantaneous velocity commands are further refined by a real-time CBF-based safety filter and tracked by the full-order low-level robot controller. Assuming accurate tracking of velocity commands, we obtain formal guarantees of safety for the full-order system. We validate the optimality and robustness of our multistage architecture, in comparison to traditional single-stage safety filters, via a detailed Pareto analysis. We further demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of our collision avoidance methodology on multiple legged robot platforms across a variety of real-world dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [345] [Acoustic Sensing for Universal Jamming Grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00351)
*Lion Weber,Theodor Wienert,Martin Splettstößer,Alexander Koenig,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于声学传感的通用夹持器形态感知：利用夹持器软体本身作为传感器，通过声波传播和机器学习重建物体属性，保持夹持器柔顺性的同时实现高精度感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触觉传感器会损害通用夹持器的柔顺性，降低抓取性能。需要一种既能保持夹持器柔顺性又能实现精确感知的解决方案。

Method: 在夹持器腔内放置扬声器和麦克风（远离可变形膜），利用声波在夹持器和物体中的传播特性，通过机器学习重建物体属性。采用解耦声学表示学习提高对无关声学变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 传感器实现高空间分辨率：物体尺寸感知误差2.6mm，方向感知误差0.6度；抗80dBA外部噪声；材料识别准确率最高100%；16种日常物体识别准确率85.6%。在真实触觉物体分拣任务中验证，实现53分钟不间断抓取和感知。

Conclusion: 声学传感作为形态感知形式，使夹持器软体本身成为传感器，完全保持柔顺性的同时实现精确物体属性感知。解耦声学表示学习提高鲁棒性，为柔顺夹持器提供有效的感知解决方案。

Abstract: Universal jamming grippers excel at grasping unknown objects due to their compliant bodies. Traditional tactile sensors can compromise this compliance, reducing grasping performance. We present acoustic sensing as a form of morphological sensing, where the gripper's soft body itself becomes the sensor. A speaker and microphone are placed inside the gripper cavity, away from the deformable membrane, fully preserving compliance. Sound propagates through the gripper and object, encoding object properties, which are then reconstructed via machine learning. Our sensor achieves high spatial resolution in sensing object size (2.6 mm error) and orientation (0.6 deg error), remains robust to external noise levels of 80 dBA, and discriminates object materials (up to 100% accuracy) and 16 everyday objects (85.6% accuracy). We validate the sensor in a realistic tactile object sorting task, achieving 53 minutes of uninterrupted grasping and sensing, confirming the preserved grasping performance. Finally, we demonstrate that disentangled acoustic representations can be learned, improving robustness to irrelevant acoustic variations.

</details>


### [346] [TMR-VLA:Vision-Language-Action Model for Magnetic Motion Control of Tri-leg Silicone-based Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00420)
*Ruijie Tang,Chi Kit Ng,Kaixuan Wu,Long Bai,Guankun Wang,Yiming Huang,Yupeng Wang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种三足磁驱动软体机器人(TMR)及其端到端多模态控制系统TMR-VLA，该系统通过融合视觉帧和自然语言指令来生成电压控制信号，实现混合运动模式的导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁驱动软体机器人在体内环境中具有无线操作和精确控制的优势，有望实现无痛检测和治疗程序。然而，现有系统在运动类型描述与底层电压控制之间存在鸿沟，需要开发能够根据语言约束的运动类型自适应调整形状的导航能力。

Method: 开发了三足磁驱动软体机器人(TMR)，采用多足设计实现更灵活步态和多样运动模式。提出TMR-VLA端到端多模态系统，融合序列帧和自然语言指令作为输入，基于当前观测状态和特定运动类型描述生成底层电压输出。系统集成了EndoVLA的内窥镜定位能力。

Result: TMR-VLA能够预测施加在TMR上的电压如何改变硅制软体机器人的动力学行为。系统达到了74%的平均成功率，验证了其通过语言约束的运动类型实现导航能力的可行性。

Conclusion: TMR-VLA系统成功地将自然语言指令与底层电压控制相连接，为磁驱动软体机器人开发了基于语言约束运动类型的导航能力，展示了在体内环境中实现灵活、自适应运动的潜力。

Abstract: In-vivo environments, magnetically actuated soft robots offer advantages such as wireless operation and precise control, showing promising potential for painless detection and therapeutic procedures. We developed a trileg magnetically driven soft robot (TMR) whose multi-legged design enables more flexible gaits and diverse motion patterns. For the silicone made of reconfigurable soft robots, its navigation ability can be separated into sequential motions, namely squatting, rotation, lifting a leg, walking and so on. Its motion and behavior depend on its bending shapes. To bridge motion type description and specific low-level voltage control, we introduced TMR-VLA, an end-to-end multi-modal system for a trileg magnetic soft robot capable of performing hybrid motion types, which is promising for developing a navigation ability by adapting its shape to language-constrained motion types. The TMR-VLA deploys embodied endoluminal localization ability from EndoVLA, and fuses sequential frames and natural language commands as input. Low-level voltage output is generated based on the current observation state and specific motion type description. The result shows the TMR-VLA can predict how the voltage applied to TMR will change the dynamics of a silicon-made soft robot. The TMR-VLA reached a 74% average success rate.

</details>


### [347] [HydroShear: Hydroelastic Shear Simulation for Tactile Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00446)
*An Dang,Jayjun Lee,Mustafa Mukadam,X. Alice Wu,Bernadette Bucher,Manikantan Nambi,Nima Fazeli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HydroShear：一种非完整流体弹性触觉模拟器，通过建模粘滑转换、路径依赖的力与剪切力积累以及完整的SE(3)物体-传感器交互，显著提升了触觉模拟的真实性，实现了零样本从仿真到现实的策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉模拟方法主要关注视觉传感器和图像渲染质量，对力和剪切力的建模过于简化，导致在灵巧操作任务中存在较大的仿真与现实差距。需要更精确的触觉物理模型来支持有效的仿真到现实策略迁移。

Method: 提出HydroShear非完整流体弹性触觉模拟器，扩展了流体弹性接触模型，使用有符号距离函数(SDFs)追踪压头与传感器膜物理交互过程中表面点的位移。该方法从任意水密几何体生成基于物理、计算高效的力场，且与底层物理引擎无关。

Result: 在GelSight Mini传感器实验中，HydroShear比现有方法更准确地再现真实触觉剪切力。在四个任务（销钉插入、装箱、书架插入、抽屉拉动）中实现了零样本仿真到现实策略迁移，平均成功率93%，显著优于基于触觉图像训练的策略(34%)和其他剪切模拟方法(58%-61%)。

Conclusion: HydroShear通过精确建模触觉物理特性，特别是剪切力和接触动力学，显著缩小了触觉仿真与现实之间的差距，为接触丰富的灵巧操作任务提供了有效的仿真到现实策略迁移解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of tactile sim-to-real policy transfer for contact-rich tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on vision-based sensors and emphasize image rendering quality while providing overly simplistic models of force and shear. Consequently, these models exhibit a large sim-to-real gap for many dexterous tasks. Here, we present HydroShear, a non-holonomic hydroelastic tactile simulator that advances the state-of-the-art by modeling: a) stick-slip transitions, b) path-dependent force and shear build up, and c) full SE(3) object-sensor interactions. HydroShear extends hydroelastic contact models using Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) to track the displacements of the on-surface points of an indenter during physical interaction with the sensor membrane. Our approach generates physics-based, computationally efficient force fields from arbitrary watertight geometries while remaining agnostic to the underlying physics engine. In experiments with GelSight Minis, HydroShear more faithfully reproduces real tactile shear compared to existing methods. This fidelity enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of reinforcement learning policies across four tasks: peg insertion, bin packing, book shelving for insertion, and drawer pulling for fine gripper control under slip. Our method achieves a 93% average success rate, outperforming policies trained on tactile images (34%) and alternative shear simulation methods (58%-61%).

</details>


### [348] [Test-Driven Agentic Framework for Reliable Robot Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00455)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Reza Akbarian Bafghi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个测试驱动的智能体框架，用于合成可部署的低层机器人导航控制器，通过迭代测试反馈优化代码


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的一次性控制器生成方法在初始提示不明确时效果不佳，需要更可靠的控制器合成方法以提高导航任务的鲁棒性

Method: 采用测试驱动的智能体框架，基于2D地图或3D仿真环境，通过结构化测试套件获取诊断反馈，使用双层修复策略（提示级细化和直接代码编辑）迭代优化控制器代码

Result: 实验结果表明，测试驱动合成相比一次性控制器生成显著提高了控制器的可靠性和鲁棒性，特别是在初始提示不明确的情况下

Conclusion: 测试驱动的控制器合成框架能有效提高机器人导航控制器的质量和可靠性，为实际部署提供了可行方案

Abstract: In this work, we present a test-driven, agentic framework for synthesizing a deployable low-level robot controller for navigation tasks. Given a 2D map with an image of an ultrasonic sensor-based robot, or a 3D robotic simulation environment, our framework iteratively refines the generated controller code using diagnostic feedback from structured test suites to achieve task success. We propose a dual-tier repair strategy to refine the generated code that alternates between prompt-level refinement and direct code editing. We evaluate the approach across 2D navigation tasks and 3D navigation in the Webots simulator. Experimental results show that test-driven synthesis substantially improves controller reliability and robustness over one-shot controller generation, especially when the initial prompt is underspecified. The source code and demonstration videos are available at: https://shivanshutripath.github.io/robotic_controller.github.io.

</details>


### [349] [Zero-Shot Robotic Manipulation via 3D Gaussian Splatting-Enhanced Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00500)
*Zilong Xie,Jingyu Gong,Xin Tan,Zhizhong Zhang,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RobMRAG：基于3D高斯溅射增强的多模态检索增强生成框架，用于零样本机器人操作，通过构建多源知识库和层次化检索策略，结合3D感知姿态优化，显著提升未见物体的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端机器人操作方法在泛化到未见物体或任务时存在局限性，主要原因是数据不足和可解释性差。虽然多模态大语言模型具有强大的常识推理能力，但在几何和空间理解方面存在不足，难以准确预测姿态。

Method: 提出RobMRAG框架：1) 构建包含物体接触帧、任务完成帧和姿态参数的多源操作知识库；2) 层次化多模态检索模块采用三级优先级混合检索策略，先检索任务相关物体原型，再基于像素级相似度和实例匹配距离选择几何最接近的参考示例；3) 引入基于3D高斯溅射的3D感知姿态优化模块，在3D空间中对齐参考物体与目标物体的姿态，并将对齐结果重投影到图像平面作为MLLM输入。

Result: 在包含30类家庭物体的测试集上，相比最佳零样本基线方法成功率提升7.76%，相比最先进的监督基线方法提升6.54%。验证了RobMRAG能有效弥合高层语义推理与低层几何执行之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: RobMRAG框架通过结合多模态检索增强生成和3D高斯溅射技术，实现了对未见物体的泛化能力，同时保持了系统的内在可解释性，为机器人操作提供了有效的零样本解决方案。

Abstract: Existing end-to-end approaches of robotic manipulation often lack generalization to unseen objects or tasks due to limited data and poor interpretability. While recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate strong commonsense reasoning, they struggle with geometric and spatial understanding required for pose prediction. In this paper, we propose RobMRAG, a 3D Gaussian Splatting-Enhanced Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MRAG) framework for zero-shot robotic manipulation. Specifically, we construct a multi-source manipulation knowledge base containing object contact frames, task completion frames, and pose parameters. During inference, a Hierarchical Multimodal Retrieval module first employs a three-priority hybrid retrieval strategy to find task-relevant object prototypes, then selects the geometrically closest reference example based on pixel-level similarity and Instance Matching Distance (IMD). We further introduce a 3D-Aware Pose Refinement module based on 3D Gaussian Splatting into the MRAG framework, which aligns the pose of the reference object to the target object in 3D space. The aligned results are reprojected onto the image plane and used as input to the MLLM to enhance the generation of the final pose parameters. Extensive experiments show that on a test set containing 30 categories of household objects, our method improves the success rate by 7.76% compared to the best-performing zero-shot baseline under the same setting, and by 6.54% compared to the state-of-the-art supervised baseline. Our results validate that RobMRAG effectively bridges the gap between high-level semantic reasoning and low-level geometric execution, enabling robotic systems that generalize to unseen objects while remaining inherently interpretable.

</details>


### [350] [Optimal-Horizon Social Robot Navigation in Heterogeneous Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00507)
*Jiamin Shi,Haolin Zhang,Yuchen Yan,Shitao Chen,Jingmin Xin,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优预测时域的社会导航框架，通过在线优化MPC预测时域来适应社交环境变化，解决传统MPC固定时域在动态人群中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC在密集动态人群导航中面临挑战：固定预测时域无法适应环境变化和社交动态，将行人视为同质障碍物忽略社交异质性，导致部分可观测环境中的"冻结机器人问题"。

Method: 提出最优时域社会导航框架：1) 时空Transformer从局部轨迹观测推断行人合作属性作为社交先验；2) 强化学习策略根据任务目标在线优化选择预测时域；3) 时域感知MPC结合社交条件安全约束平衡导航效率与交互安全。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验表明：相比最先进基线，成功率提高6.8%，碰撞减少50%，导航时间缩短19%，超时率仅0.8%，验证了社交最优规划时域对密集环境高效安全导航的必要性。

Conclusion: 规划时域应作为社交条件决策变量而非固定设计选择，最优时域选择对部分可观测人群中的鲁棒社会导航至关重要，所提框架在效率与安全间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Navigating social robots in dense, dynamic crowds is challenging due to environmental uncertainty and complex human-robot interactions. While Model Predictive Control (MPC) offers strong real-time performance, its reliance on a fixed prediction horizon limits adaptability to changing environments and social dynamics. Furthermore, most MPC approaches treat pedestrians as homogeneous obstacles, ignoring social heterogeneity and cooperative or adversarial interactions, which often causes the Frozen Robot Problem in partially observable real-world environments. In this paper, we identify the planning horizon as a socially conditioned decision variable rather than a fixed design choice. Building on this insight, we propose an optimal-horizon social navigation framework that optimizes MPC foresight online according to inferred social context. A spatio-temporal Transformer infers pedestrian cooperation attributes from local trajectory observations, which serve as social priors for a reinforcement learning policy that optimally selects the prediction horizon under a task-driven objective. The resulting horizon-aware MPC incorporates socially conditioned safety constraints to balance navigation efficiency and interaction safety. Extensive simulations and real-world robot experiments demonstrate that optimal foresight selection is critical for robust social navigation in partially observable crowds. Compared to state-of-the-art baselines, the proposed approach achieves a 6.8\% improvement in success rate, reduces collisions by 50\%, and shortens navigation time by 19\%, with a low timeout rate of 0.8\%, validating the necessity of socially optimal planning horizons for efficient and safe robot navigation in crowded environments. Code and videos are available at Under Review.

</details>


### [351] [Planning Method for Skill-Based Control of Robots Using a PLC as Skill Trigger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00555)
*Andreas Gaugenrieder,Hari Hara Balasubramaniam,Jannik Möhrle,Rüdiger Daub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集成运动序列优化的技能型机器人编程方法，通过MoveContinuousSkill实现执行时间显著提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能型机器人编程方案忽视运动序列优化，导致执行效率低下，需要改进

Method: 引入集成运动序列优化的规划方法，创建MoveContinuousSkill，在可编程逻辑控制器上实现软件并应用于多个机器人系统

Result: 实验结果显示通过优化运动序列，执行时间得到显著改善

Conclusion: 提出的方法成功提升了技能型机器人编程的执行效率，MoveContinuousSkill在多个机器人系统中有效应用

Abstract: Skill-based programming of robots provides a flexible approach for automation. Existing solutions neglect the optimization of motion sequences, leading to inefficiencies in execution. This work introduces a planning method that enhances skill-based robot programming by integrating motion sequence optimization. This optimization leads to a new MoveContinuousSkill. The software for executing the MoveContinuousSkill is implemented on a Programmable Logic Controller and applied across multiple robotic systems. Experimental results demonstrate a significant improvement in execution time through optimized motion sequence.

</details>


### [352] [LangGap: Diagnosing and Closing the Language Gap in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00592)
*Yuchen Hou,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了当前最先进的VLA模型在语言理解方面的严重缺陷，构建了LangGap基准测试，通过语义扰动方法暴露模型问题，并发现数据增强只能部分解决问题，模型在处理多样化语言指令时存在根本性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型虽然在标准基准测试上达到95%以上的成功率，但通过系统实验发现这些模型很大程度上忽略了语言指令。先前研究缺乏：(1)系统的语义扰动诊断，(2)强制语言理解的基准测试设计，(3)语言多样化的训练数据。需要构建新的基准测试来揭示和解决VLA模型在语言理解方面的根本缺陷。

Method: 构建LangGap基准测试，基于四维语义扰动方法——在保持桌面布局固定的情况下改变指令语义。与现有基准（如LIBERO）每个布局只分配一个任务不同，LangGap在相同布局下完全多样化拾放任务，强制模型真正理解语言。通过目标数据增强进行实验验证。

Result: 目标数据增强可以部分缩小语言理解差距——单任务训练下成功率从0%提升到90%，多任务训练下从0%提升到28%。但随着扩展任务语义多样性的增加，模型学习能力严重不足；即使是训练过的任务也表现不佳。这揭示了VLA模型在理解多样化语言指令方面的根本挑战。

Conclusion: 当前VLA模型在语言理解方面存在严重缺陷，LangGap基准测试通过语义扰动方法有效暴露了这一问题。虽然数据增强能部分改善性能，但模型在处理多样化语言指令时的学习能力严重不足，这构成了VLA模型发展的根本挑战，也是LangGap基准的长期价值所在。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve over 95% success on standard benchmarks. However, through systematic experiments, we find that current state-of-the-art VLA models largely ignore language instructions. Prior work lacks: (1) systematic semantic perturbation diagnostics, (2) a benchmark that forces language understanding by design, and (3) linguistically diverse training data.
  This paper constructs the LangGap benchmark, based on a four-dimensional semantic perturbation method -- varying instruction semantics while keeping the tabletop layout fixed -- revealing language understanding deficits in π0.5. Existing benchmarks like LIBERO assign only one task per layout, underutilizing available objects and target locations; LangGap fully diversifies pick-and-place tasks under identical layouts, forcing models to truly understand language.
  Experiments show that targeted data augmentation can partially close the language gap -- success rate improves from 0% to 90% with single-task training, and 0% to 28% with multi-task training. However, as semantic diversity of extended tasks increases, model learning capacity proves severely insufficient; even trained tasks perform poorly. This reveals a fundamental challenge for VLA models in understanding diverse language instructions -- precisely the long-term value of LangGap.

</details>


### [353] [AI-IO: An Aerodynamics-Inspired Real-Time Inertial Odometry for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00597)
*Jiahao Cui,Feng Yu,Linzuo Zhang,Yu Hu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于转子转速测量和Transformer架构的惯性里程计学习框架，通过结合物理模型和深度学习，显著提高了四旋翼飞行器的速度预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型惯性里程计系统要么仅使用IMU数据，要么包含推力等额外动态相关输入，但缺乏对底层物理模型的原则性建模。这种缺乏可解释性限制了模型的泛化能力和准确性。

Method: 受空气动力学模型和IMU测量模型启发，识别出惯性里程计所需的关键物理量——转子转速测量，并设计基于Transformer的惯性里程计架构。该架构能更有效地利用时间依赖性进行去噪和空气动力学建模，最后结合不确定性感知的扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行验证。

Result: 通过引入转子转速测量，模型将速度预测精度提高了36.9%。Transformer架构进一步带来22.4%的精度提升。在多个数据集和实时系统上的验证表明，该框架具有优越的准确性、泛化能力和实时性能。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合物理模型和深度学习，提出了一种改进的四旋翼惯性里程计方法，显著提高了精度和泛化能力，并提供了开源代码和数据集以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Inertial Odometry (IO) has gained attention in quadrotor applications due to its sole reliance on inertial measurement units (IMUs), attributed to its lightweight design, low cost, and robust performance across diverse environments. However, most existing learning-based inertial odometry systems for quadrotors either use only IMU data or include additional dynamics-related inputs such as thrust, but still lack a principled formulation of the underlying physical model to be learned. This lack of interpretability hampers the model's ability to generalize and often limits its accuracy. In this work, we approach the inertial odometry learning problem from a different perspective. Inspired by the aerodynamics model and IMU measurement model, we identify the key physical quantity--rotor speed measurements required for inertial odometry and design a transformer-based inertial odometry. By incorporating rotor speed measurements, the proposed model improves velocity prediction accuracy by 36.9%. Furthermore, the transformer architecture more effectively exploits temporal dependencies for denoising and aerodynamics modeling, yielding an additional 22.4% accuracy gain over previous results. To support evaluation, we also provide a real-world quadrotor flight dataset capturing IMU measurements and rotor speed for high-speed motion. Finally, combined with an uncertainty-aware extended Kalman filter (EKF), our framework is validated across multiple datasets and real-time systems, demonstrating superior accuracy, generalization, and real-time performance. We share the code and data to promote further research (https://github.com/SJTU-ViSYS-team/AI-IO).

</details>


### [354] [I-Perceive: A Foundation Model for Active Perception with Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00600)
*Yongxi Huang,Zhuohang Wang,Wenjing Tang,Cewu Lu,Panpan Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: I-Perceive是一个用于移动机械臂在室内环境中进行主动感知的基础模型，能够根据自然语言指令预测相机视角，融合视觉语言模型和几何基础模型，实现语义与几何理解相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法主要局限于局部场景（如桌面环境）和固定感知目标（如减少遮挡），无法处理大规模环境中开放意图的主动感知问题。需要开发能够根据开放语言指令在大规模环境中进行主动感知的解决方案。

Method: 提出I-Perceive模型，将视觉语言模型（VLM）骨干与几何基础模型融合，基于图像场景上下文预测符合语言指令的相机视角。使用包含真实世界场景扫描数据和仿真数据的多样化数据集进行训练，通过自动化可扩展的数据生成流程处理数据。

Result: 实验表明，I-Perceive在生成相机视角的预测准确性和指令跟随方面显著优于最先进的视觉语言模型，并在新场景和新任务上表现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: I-Perceive成功解决了大规模环境中开放意图主动感知的挑战，通过融合语义和几何理解，为移动机械臂在室内环境中的主动感知提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Active perception, the ability of a robot to proactively adjust its viewpoint to acquire task-relevant information, is essential for robust operation in unstructured real-world environments. While critical for downstream tasks such as manipulation, existing approaches have largely been confined to local settings (e.g., table-top scenes) with fixed perception objectives (e.g., occlusion reduction). Addressing active perception with open-ended intents in large-scale environments remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose I-Perceive, a foundation model for active perception conditioned on natural language instructions, designed for mobile manipulators and indoor environments. I-Perceive predicts camera views that follows open-ended language instructions, based on image-based scene contexts. By fusing a Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone with a geometric foundation model, I-Perceive bridges semantic and geometric understanding, thus enabling effective reasoning for active perception. We train I-Perceive on a diverse dataset comprising real-world scene-scanning data and simulation data, both processed via an automated and scalable data generation pipeline. Experiments demonstrate that I-Perceive significantly outperforms state-of-the-art VLMs in both prediction accuracy and instruction following of generated camera views, and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to novel scenes and tasks.

</details>


### [355] [TGM-VLA: Task-Guided Mixup for Sampling-Efficient and Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00615)
*Fanqi Pu,Lei Jiang,Wenming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个改进机器人模仿学习的整体框架，通过优化关键帧采样、添加颜色反转投影分支和任务引导混合技术，显著提升模型性能和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模仿学习方法存在关键帧冗余、时间分布不平衡导致内存使用效率低和优化不稳定，以及点云重投影到黑色背景图像时暗色物体难以区分的问题。

Method: 1) 重新设计和优化关键帧采样策略，减少80%内存消耗并加速训练5倍；2) 添加颜色反转投影分支解决暗色物体模糊问题；3) 提出任务引导混合技术，根据任务指令动态融合点云和动作热图。

Result: 在RLBench上达到90.5%的成功率，在COLOSSEUM基准测试的干扰条件下达到68.8%，实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的整体框架通过优化数据采样、增强视觉表示和引入任务引导混合，显著提升了机器人模仿学习的性能和训练效率，为解决关键帧冗余和暗色物体识别问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The performance of robotic imitation learning is fundamentally limited by data quality and training strategies. Prevalent sampling strategies on RLBench suffer from severe keyframe redundancy and imbalanced temporal distribution, leading to inefficient memory usage and unstable optimization. Moreover, reprojecting point clouds onto multi-view images with a black background--while more efficient than voxel-based methods--often causes dark objects to be indistinguishable and hard to manipulate. In this work, we propose a novel holistic framework that significantly improves both model performance and training efficiency. First, we redesign and optimize the keyframe sampling strategy, reducing memory consumption by 80% and accelerating training speed by 5x. Second, we augment the model with a color inversion projection branch--a simple yet effective module that resolves the ambiguity of dark objects. Finally, we propose a task-guided mixup technique that dynamically fuses point clouds and action heatmaps according to task instructions, greatly improving robustness to distractors and performance in multi-goal scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with a 90.5% success rate on RLBench and 68.8% on the COLOSSEUM benchmark under challenging interference conditions. Our code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/PuFanqi23/TGM-VLA.

</details>


### [356] [Validation of Space Robotics in Underwater Environments via Disturbance Robustness Equivalency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00628)
*Joris Verhagen,Elias Krantz,Chelsea Sidrane,David Dörner,Nicola De Carli,Pedro Roque,Huina Mao,Gunnar Tibert,Ivan Stenius,Christer Fuglesang,Dimos Dimarogonas,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于水下环境的太空机器人验证框架，利用中性浮力模拟微重力，通过信号时序逻辑和最大扰动鲁棒性确保太空任务与验证任务具有相同的鲁棒性程度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境虽然能近似模拟微重力条件，但存在动力学和环境差异，需要开发有效的验证框架来确保太空机器人任务在地面验证中的可靠性

Method: 使用信号时序逻辑表达高级太空任务规范，通过最大扰动鲁棒性概念克服环境差异，设计运动规划使太空任务和验证任务达到相同的扰动鲁棒性程度，采用近相同的控制策略，并在执行过程中估计扰动并与鲁棒性程度比较

Result: 建立双实验验证设置：水下机器人在近中性浮力条件下运行，验证实验性平面航天器平台或CubeSat在高保真太空动力学模拟器中的规划与控制策略

Conclusion: 提出的验证框架通过扰动鲁棒性匹配和闭环控制策略，为太空机器人任务提供了有效的地面验证方法，能够评估在太空环境中的实际运行能力

Abstract: We present an experimental validation framework for space robotics that leverages underwater environments to approximate microgravity dynamics. While neutral buoyancy conditions make underwater robotics an excellent platform for space robotics validation, there are still dynamical and environmental differences that need to be overcome. Given a high-level space mission specification, expressed in terms of a Signal Temporal Logic specification, we overcome these differences via the notion of maximal disturbance robustness of the mission. We formulate the motion planning problem such that the original space mission and the validation mission achieve the same disturbance robustness degree. The validation platform then executes its mission plan using a near-identical control strategy to the space mission where the closed-loop controller considers the spacecraft dynamics. Evaluating our validation framework relies on estimating disturbances during execution and comparing them to the disturbance robustness degree, providing practical evidence of operation in the space environment. Our evaluation features a dual-experiment setup: an underwater robot operating under near-neutral buoyancy conditions to validate the planning and control strategy of either an experimental planar spacecraft platform or a CubeSat in a high-fidelity space dynamics simulator.

</details>


### [357] [Optimal Solutions for the Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem via Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00663)
*Anoop Bhat,Geordan Gutow,Zhongqiang Ren,Sivakumar Rathinam,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出精确算法BPRC解决移动目标车辆路径问题，通过新型标签算法和定制化支配准则处理移动目标和时变旅行成本，相比基线方法快一个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MT-VRP涉及多个智能体拦截移动目标的轨迹规划，存在速度、时间窗和容量约束。现有分支定价方法面临定价子问题的挑战，主要困难在于移动目标和目标间时变旅行成本带来的复杂性

Method: 提出BPRC（带松弛连续性的分支定价）精确算法，核心贡献是新型标签算法，采用专门为移动目标问题设计的支配准则来解决定价子问题

Result: 在最多25个目标的实例上，算法比基于先前工作的基线方法快一个数量级找到最优解，在智能体容量受限的场景中表现尤为突出

Conclusion: BPRC算法通过创新的标签算法和支配准则有效解决了MT-VRP的定价子问题挑战，显著提升了求解效率，特别是在容量受限场景中表现出色

Abstract: The Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem (MT-VRP) seeks trajectories for several agents that intercept a set of moving targets, subject to speed, time window, and capacity constraints. We introduce an exact algorithm, Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (BPRC), for the MT-VRP. The main challenge in a branch-and-price approach for the MT-VRP is the pricing subproblem, which is complicated by moving targets and time-dependent travel costs between targets. Our key contribution is a new labeling algorithm that solves this subproblem by means of a novel dominance criterion tailored for problems with moving targets. Numerical results on instances with up to 25 targets show that our algorithm finds optimal solutions more than an order of magnitude faster than a baseline based on previous work, showing particular strength in scenarios with limited agent capacities.

</details>


### [358] [Wild-Drive: Off-Road Scene Captioning and Path Planning via Robust Multi-modal Routing and Efficient Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00694)
*Zihang Wang,Xu Li,Benwu Wang,Wenkai Zhu,Xieyuanli Chen,Dong Kong,Kailin Lyu,Yinan Du,Yiming Peng,Haoyang Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Wild-Drive是一个用于越野场景描述和路径规划的框架，通过多模态编码器和任务条件模态路由桥（MoRo-Former）自适应聚合退化感知下的可靠信息，结合高效LLM生成结构化描述并预测轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景描述方法主要针对结构化城市场景，在越野环境中容易受到雨、雾、雪、黑暗等单模态退化影响，且缺乏统一框架联合建模结构化场景描述和路径规划。

Method: 采用现代多模态编码器，引入任务条件模态路由桥MoRo-Former自适应聚合退化感知下的可靠信息，集成高效LLM、规划令牌和GRU解码器生成结构化描述并预测未来轨迹。

Result: 在OR-C2P数据集和自收集数据集上的实验表明，Wild-Drive优于先前基于LLM的方法，在退化感知条件下保持更高的稳定性。

Conclusion: Wild-Drive为越野环境提供了一种高效的场景描述和路径规划框架，通过自适应模态聚合解决了感知退化问题，提高了自动驾驶系统的透明度和安全性。

Abstract: Explainability and transparent decision-making are essential for the safe deployment of autonomous driving systems. Scene captioning summarizes environmental conditions and risk factors in natural language, improving transparency, safety, and human--robot interaction. However, most existing approaches target structured urban scenarios; in off-road environments, they are vulnerable to single-modality degradations caused by rain, fog, snow, and darkness, and they lack a unified framework that jointly models structured scene captioning and path planning. To bridge this gap, we propose Wild-Drive, an efficient framework for off-road scene captioning and path planning. Wild-Drive adopts modern multimodal encoders and introduces a task-conditioned modality-routing bridge, MoRo-Former, to adaptively aggregate reliable information under degraded sensing. It then integrates an efficient large language model (LLM), together with a planning token and a gate recurrent unit (GRU) decoder, to generate structured captions and predict future trajectories. We also build the OR-C2P Benchmark, which covers structured off-road scene captioning and path planning under diverse sensor corruption conditions. Experiments on OR-C2P dataset and a self-collected dataset show that Wild-Drive outperforms prior LLM-based methods and remains more stable under degraded sensing. The code and benchmark will be publicly available at https://github.com/wangzihanggg/Wild-Drive.

</details>


### [359] [UniHM: Unified Dexterous Hand Manipulation with Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00732)
*Zhenhao Zhang,Jiaxin Liu,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniHM：首个基于自由形式语言指令的统一灵巧手操作框架，通过统一手部形态编码、视觉语言动作模型和物理引导动态优化，实现物理可行的灵巧手操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手操作规划方法通常依赖物体中心线索或精确的手-物体交互序列，缺乏开放词汇指令提供的丰富组合性指导。需要一种能够理解自由形式语言指令并生成物理可行操作序列的框架。

Method: 1. 统一灵巧手分词器：将异构的灵巧手形态映射到共享码本，提高跨手部形态泛化能力；2. 视觉语言动作模型：仅使用人-物体交互数据训练，无需大规模遥操作数据集；3. 物理引导动态细化模块：在生成和时间先验下进行分段联合优化，确保物理可行性。

Result: 在多个数据集和真实世界评估中，UniHM在已见和未见物体及轨迹上均达到最先进水平，展示了强大的泛化能力和高物理可行性。

Conclusion: UniHM是首个能够根据自由形式语言指令生成物理可行灵巧手操作序列的统一框架，通过统一手部形态编码和物理引导优化，实现了对开放词汇指令的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Planning physically feasible dexterous hand manipulation is a central challenge in robotic manipulation and Embodied AI. Prior work typically relies on object-centric cues or precise hand-object interaction sequences, foregoing the rich, compositional guidance of open-vocabulary instruction. We introduce UniHM, the first framework for unified dexterous hand manipulation guided by free-form language commands. We propose a Unified Hand-Dexterous Tokenizer that maps heterogeneous dexterous-hand morphologies into a single shared codebook, improving cross-dexterous hand generalization and scalability to new morphologies. Our vision language action model is trained solely on human-object interaction data, eliminating the need for massive real-world teleoperation datasets, and demonstrates strong generalizability in producing human-like manipulation sequences from open-ended language instructions. To ensure physical realism, we introduce a physics-guided dynamic refinement module that performs segment-wise joint optimization under generative and temporal priors, yielding smooth and physically feasible manipulation sequences. Across multiple datasets and real-world evaluations, UniHM attains state-of-the-art results on both seen and unseen objects and trajectories, demonstrating strong generalization and high physical feasibility. Our project page at \href{https://unihm.github.io/}{https://unihm.github.io/}.

</details>


### [360] [Online Generation of Collision-Free Trajectories in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00759)
*Nermin Covic,Bakir Lacevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线方法，可将任意几何路径（由各种规划器生成）转换为运动学可行、加加速度受限的轨迹，支持实时适应环境变化和重新规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器生成的几何路径通常不考虑运动学约束（如加加速度限制），无法直接用于机器人控制。需要一种方法能够在线地将任意路径转换为运动学可行的轨迹，同时支持实时适应动态环境变化。

Method: 使用五次/四次样条序列将几何路径转换为轨迹，可离散化为用户指定的控制频率并流式传输给底层控制器。方法支持实时重新调用，从机器人当前状态到目标状态生成新轨迹，并保证动态环境中一定时间内的无碰撞性。

Result: 在对比仿真研究中，该方法在平滑性、计算时间和实时性能方面优于竞争方法，特别是在目标状态频繁变化（高达1kHz）的场景中。真实机器人实验验证了该方法在包括人类存在在内的实际场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效地将任意几何路径转换为运动学可行、加加速度受限的轨迹，支持实时适应环境变化，在动态环境中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an online method for converting an arbitrary geometric path represented by a sequence of states, generated by any planner (e.g., sampling-based planners like RRT or PRM, search-based planners like ARA*, etc.), into a corresponding kinematically feasible, jerk-limited trajectory. The method generates a sequence of quintic/quartic splines that can be discretized at a user-specified control rate, and then streamed to a low-level robot controller. Our approach enables real-time adaptation to newly captured changes in the environment. It can also be re-invoked at any time instance to generate a new trajectory from the robot's current to a desired target state or sequence of states. We can guarantee that the trajectory will remain collision-free for a certain amount of time in dynamic environments, while allowing bounded geometric deviation from the original path. The kinematic constraints are taken into account, including limited jerk. We validate the approach in a comparative simulation study against the competing method, demonstrating favorable behavior w.r.t. smoothness, computational time, and real-time performance, particularly in scenarios with frequent changes of target states (up to 1 [kHz]). Experiments on a real robot demonstrate that the proposed approach can be used in real-world scenarios including human presence.

</details>


### [361] [Hippo: High-performance Interior-Point and Projection-based Solver for Generic Constrained Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00871)
*Haizhou Zhao,Ludovic Righetti,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hippo是一个用于机器人轨迹优化的新型求解器，采用内点法处理不等式约束，通过投影或内点法处理硬等式约束，相比传统SQP和DDP方法在计算效率、建模灵活性和收敛性方面有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于序列二次规划（SQP）或微分动态规划（DDP）的轨迹优化器存在计算效率慢、建模灵活性低、对需要硬约束的复杂任务收敛性差等局限性。

Method: Hippo采用内点法（IPM）处理不等式约束，结合自适应障碍更新策略；对于硬等式约束，通过投影或内点法处理，形成一种鲁棒且高效的轨迹优化求解器。

Result: 通过大量数值基准测试表明，Hippo对于需要高质量解的困难机器人轨迹优化问题（如运动控制和操作任务）是一个鲁棒且高效的替代方案，优于现有最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: Hippo为解决复杂机器人轨迹优化问题提供了一个有效的工具，特别是在需要处理硬约束和高精度解的场景下，相比传统方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization is the core of modern model-based robotic control and motion planning. Existing trajectory optimizers, based on sequential quadratic programming (SQP) or differential dynamic programming (DDP), are often limited by their slow computation efficiency, low modeling flexibility, and poor convergence for complex tasks requiring hard constraints. In this paper, we introduce Hippo, a solver that can handle inequality constraints using the interior-point method (IPM) with an adaptive barrier update strategy and hard equality constraints via projection or IPM. Through extensive numerical benchmarks, we show that Hippo is a robust and efficient alternative to existing state-of-the-art solvers for difficult robotic trajectory optimization problems requiring high-quality solutions, such as locomotion and manipulation.

</details>


### [362] [A Novel Reconfigurable Dexterous Hand Based on Triple-Symmetric Bricard Parallel Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00892)
*Chunxu Tian,Zhichao Huang,Hongzeng Li,Bo Wang,Jinghao Jia,Yirui Sun,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于并联机构的新型机械手设计，采用三重对称Bricard连杆作为可重构手掌，通过拓扑和尺寸综合实现平衡自由度配置，提升对不同形状尺寸物体的适应性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械手在适应不同形状和尺寸物体时存在局限性，需要一种能够平衡灵巧性、稳定性和负载能力的可重构手掌设计，以提高机器人操作的通用性和精确性

Method: 使用三重对称Bricard连杆作为可重构手掌，进行拓扑和尺寸综合优化自由度配置；采用螺旋理论和闭环约束进行运动学分析；基于工作空间、刚度和运动/力传递效率评估性能；开发原型并进行抓取实验验证

Result: 设计实现了平衡的自由度和连杆配置，原型测试表明能够稳定高效地抓取多种物体，验证了设计在提升抓取通用性和操作精度方面的有效性

Conclusion: 基于并联机构和可重构Bricard手掌的机械手设计为高级机器人操作任务提供了有前景的解决方案，显著提高了抓取适应性和操作性能

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel design for a robotic hand based on parallel mechanisms. The proposed hand uses a triple-symmetric Bricard linkage as its reconfigurable palm, enhancing adaptability to objects of varying shapes and sizes. Through topological and dimensional synthesis, the mechanism achieves a well-balanced degree of freedom and link configuration suitable for reconfigurable palm motion, balancing dexterity, stability, and load capacity. Furthermore, kinematic analysis is performed using screw theory and closed-loop constraints, and performance is evaluated based on workspace, stiffness, and motion/force transmission efficiency. Finally, a prototype is developed and tested through a series of grasping experiments, demonstrating the ability to perform stable and efficient manipulation across a wide range of objects. The results validate the effectiveness of the design in improving grasping versatility and operational precision, offering a promising solution for advanced robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [363] [Minimalist Compliance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00913)
*Haochen Shi,Songbo Hu,Yifan Hou,Weizhuo Wang,Karen Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用电机电流/电压信号实现柔顺控制的方法，无需力传感器、电流控制或学习，通过雅可比矩阵估计外力并集成到任务空间导纳控制器中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔顺控制对安全物理交互至关重要，但受限于力扭矩传感器等硬件需求。现有强化学习方法存在仿真到现实的差距、缺乏安全保证和系统复杂性问题，需要更简单可靠的解决方案。

Method: 提出Minimalist Compliance Control方法，仅利用现代伺服和准直驱电机中可用的电机电流或电压信号，通过雅可比矩阵从执行器信号估计外部力矩，并集成到任务空间导纳控制器中。

Result: 在机器人手臂、灵巧手和两个人形机器人上验证了该方法，在多种接触丰富的任务中表现出鲁棒、安全和柔顺的交互特性，兼容视觉语言模型、模仿学习和基于模型的规划等多种高层规划器。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了仅使用电机信号的无传感器柔顺控制，具有平台无关性和即插即用特性，为安全物理交互提供了简单可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Compliance control is essential for safe physical interaction, yet its adoption is limited by hardware requirements such as force torque sensors. While recent reinforcement learning approaches aim to bypass these constraints, they often suffer from sim-to-real gaps, lack safety guarantees, and add system complexity. We propose Minimalist Compliance Control, which enables compliant behavior using only motor current or voltage signals readily available in modern servos and quasi-direct-drive motors, without force sensors, current control, or learning. External wrenches are estimated from actuator signals and Jacobians and incorporated into a task-space admittance controller, preserving sufficient force measurement accuracy for stable and responsive compliance control. Our method is embodiment-agnostic and plug-and-play with diverse high-level planners. We validate our approach on a robot arm, a dexterous hand, and two humanoid robots across multiple contact-rich tasks, using vision-language models, imitation learning, and model-based planning. The results demonstrate robust, safe, and compliant interaction across embodiments and planning paradigms.

</details>


### [364] [DAM-VLA: A Dynamic Action Model-Based Vision-Language-Action Framework for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00926)
*Xiongfeng Peng,Jiaqian Yu,Dingzhe Li,Yixiang Jin,Lu Xu,Yamin Mao,Chao Zhang,Weiming Li,Sujin Jang,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAM-VLA：一种基于动态动作模型的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过路由机制和双尺度动作加权，在动态环境中实现粗大运动与精细操作的协调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于预训练视觉语言模型的VLA框架难以平衡通用任务适应性与精细操作所需的专门精度，在仓库、医院、家庭等动态环境中，机器人需要在粗大运动和精确操作间无缝切换

Method: 提出DAM-VLA框架，整合VLM推理与基于扩散的动作模型，包含：(1)基于任务特定视觉和语言线索的动作路由机制；(2)融合高层VLM认知与低层视觉特征的动态动作模型；(3)实现臂部运动和抓取操作模型动态协调的双尺度动作加权机制

Result: 在模拟环境（SIMPLER、FurnitureBench）和真实世界设置中，DAM-VLA相比最先进的VLA方法取得了更高的成功率，从标准拾放任务到具有挑战性的长视野和接触密集型任务都表现出强大的泛化能力

Conclusion: DAM-VLA通过动态动作模型和协调机制，有效解决了VLA框架中通用适应性与操作精度之间的平衡问题，为动态环境中的机器人复杂任务执行提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: In dynamic environments such as warehouses, hospitals, and homes, robots must seamlessly transition between gross motion and precise manipulations to complete complex tasks. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks, largely adapted from pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs), often struggle to reconcile general task adaptability with the specialized precision required for intricate manipulation. To address this challenge, we propose DAM-VLA, a dynamic action model-based VLA framework. DAM-VLA integrates VLM reasoning with diffusion-based action models specialized for arm and gripper control. Specifically, it introduces (i) an action routing mechanism, using task-specific visual and linguistic cues to select appropriate action models (e.g., arm movement or gripper manipulation), (ii) a dynamic action model that fuses high-level VLM cognition with low-level visual features to predict actions, and (iii) a dual-scale action weighting mechanism that enables dynamic coordination between the arm-movement and gripper-manipulation models. Across extensive evaluations, DAM-VLA achieves superior success rates compared to state-of-the-art VLA methods in simulated (SIMPLER, FurnitureBench) and real-world settings, showing robust generalization from standard pick-and-place to demanding long-horizon and contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [365] [DRIFT: Diffusion-based Rule-Inferred For Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00936)
*Jinyang Zhao,Handong Zheng,Yanjiu Zhong,Qiang Zhang,Yu Kang,Shunyu Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT是一个基于条件扩散的轨迹生成框架，通过整合关系归纳偏置和时间注意力偏置，在非结构化环境中为移动机器人生成既平滑又精确的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在非结构化环境中的轨迹生成面临关键困境：需要在保证安全执行的运动学平滑性与精细任务的终端精度之间取得平衡。现有生成式规划器往往难以兼顾这两者，要么生成平滑但不精确的路径，要么生成几何准确但运动不稳定的轨迹。

Method: 提出了DRIFT（基于扩散的规则推断轨迹）条件扩散框架，整合两种互补的归纳偏置：1）关系归纳偏置：通过基于GNN的结构化场景感知模块编码全局拓扑约束，确保整体平滑性；2）时间注意力偏置：通过新颖的图条件时间感知GRU实现，动态关注稀疏障碍物和目标，实现精确的局部机动。

Result: DRIFT成功调和了平滑性与精度的冲突目标，达到了厘米级的模仿保真度（0.041m FDE）和具有竞争力的平滑性（27.19 Jerk），为下游控制提供了高度可执行的参考规划。

Conclusion: DRIFT框架通过整合关系归纳偏置和时间注意力偏置，有效解决了移动机器人在非结构化环境中轨迹生成时平滑性与精度之间的权衡问题，生成了高质量、可执行的参考轨迹。

Abstract: Trajectory generation for mobile robots in unstructured environments faces a critical dilemma: balancing kinematic smoothness for safe execution with terminal precision for fine-grained tasks. Existing generative planners often struggle with this trade-off, yielding either smooth but imprecise paths or geometrically accurate but erratic motions. To address the aforementioned shortcomings, this article proposes DRIFT (Diffusion-based Rule-Inferred for Trajectories), a conditional diffusion framework designed to generate high-fidelity reference trajectories by integrating two complementary inductive biases. First, a Relational Inductive Bias, realized via a GNN-based Structured Scene Perception (SSP) module, encodes global topological constraints to ensure holistic smoothness. Second, a Temporal Attention Bias, implemented through a novel Graph-Conditioned Time-Aware GRU (GTGRU), dynamically attends to sparse obstacles and targets for precise local maneuvering. In the end, quantitative results demonstrate that DRIFT reconciles these conflicting objectives, achieving centimeter-level imitation fidelity (0.041m FDE) and competitive smoothness (27.19 Jerk). This balance yields highly executable reference plans for downstream control.

</details>


### [366] [HierKick: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Guided Soccer Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00948)
*Yizhi Chen,Zheng Zhang,Zhanxiang Cao,Yihe Chen,Shengcheng Fu,Liyun Yan,Yang Zhang,Jiali Liu,Haoyang Li,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HierKick：基于双频分层强化学习的视觉引导足球机器人控制框架，通过5Hz高层策略和50Hz底层控制器实现多时间尺度决策，在仿真和真实环境中均取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球机器人控制涉及多时间尺度决策，需要平衡长期战术规划和短期运动执行。传统端到端强化学习方法在复杂动态环境中面临挑战，需要更有效的分层控制架构。

Method: 提出HierKick框架，采用分层控制架构：5Hz高层策略集成YOLOv8进行实时检测，通过教练模型选择任务；预训练的50Hz底层控制器进行精确关节控制。该架构实现接近、对齐、运球和踢球四个步骤。

Result: 实验结果显示，该框架在IsaacGym中成功率为95.2%，在Mujoco中为89.8%，在真实世界中为80%。证明了分层架构在复杂动态环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: HierKick为复杂环境中的机器人控制提供了有效的分层范式，可扩展到多时间尺度任务，其模块化设计和技能重用为智能机器人控制提供了新路径。

Abstract: Controlling soccer robots involves multi-time-scale decision-making, which requires balancing long-term tactical planning and short-term motion execution. Traditional end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) methods face challenges in complex dynamic environments. This paper proposes HierKick, a vision-guided soccer robot control framework based on dual-frequency hierarchical RL. The framework adopts a hierarchical control architecture featuring a 5 Hz high-level policy that integrates YOLOv8 for real-time detection and selects tasks via a coach model, and a pre-trained 50 Hz low-level controller for precise joint control. Through this architecture, the framework achieves the four steps of approaching, aligning, dribbling, and kicking. Experimental results show that the success rates of this framework are 95.2\% in IsaacGym, 89.8\% in Mujoco, and 80\% in the real world. HierKick provides an effective hierarchical paradigm for robot control in complex environments, extendable to multi-time-scale tasks, with its modular design and skill reuse offering a new path for intelligent robot control.

</details>


### [367] [MiniUGV$_2$: A Compact UAV-Deployable Tracked Ground Vehicle with Manipulation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00972)
*Durgakant Pushp,Swapnil Kalhapure,Shaekh Mohammad Shithil,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了miniUGV₂，一种可由无人机部署的紧凑型履带地面机器人，用于探索无人机难以进入的隐藏空间，通过创新的系留模块和双机械臂设计解决部署、回收和操作挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索和检查"隐藏空间"（即入口仅对空中机器人可访问但由于几何约束、有限飞行时间和通信丢失而未被探索的环境）仍然是一个重大挑战。需要扩展无人机能力进入这些受限环境。

Method: 开发了miniUGV₂，一种紧凑型无人机可部署的履带地面车辆，具有双关节臂、集成LiDAR和深度传感以及模块化电子设备。创新性地设计了带有电磁头的系留模块，可实现安全部署、回收和可选分离，克服了以往的缠绕问题。

Result: 实验证明了系统在崎岖地形导航、自我扶正和操作重达3.5公斤物体方面的鲁棒性，验证了miniUGV₂作为混合空中-地面机器人平台的通用性。

Conclusion: miniUGV₂是一个多功能平台，通过创新的系留部署机制和增强的自主能力，成功扩展了无人机进入受限环境的能力，为混合空中-地面机器人系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Exploring and inspecting \emph{Hidden Spaces}, defined as environments whose entrances are accessible only to aerial robots but remain unexplored due to geometric constraints, limited flight time, and communication loss, remains a major challenge. We present miniUGV$_2$, a compact UAV-deployable tracked ground vehicle that extends UAV capabilities into confined environments. The system introduces dual articulated arms, integrated LiDAR and depth sensing, and modular electronics for enhanced autonomy. A novel tether module with an electro-permanent magnetic head enables safe deployment, retrieval, and optional detachment, thereby overcoming prior entanglement issues. Experiments demonstrate robust terrain navigation, self-righting, and manipulation of objects up to 3.5 kg, validating miniUGV$_2$ as a versatile platform for hybrid aerial-ground robotics.

</details>


### [368] [An Open-Source Modular Benchmark for Diffusion-Based Motion Planning in Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01023)
*Yun Li,Simon Thompson,Yidu Zhang,Ehsan Javanmardi,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个开源模块化基准测试，用于评估扩散运动规划器在真实自动驾驶系统中的性能，解决了现有评估中忽略通信延迟和实时约束的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散运动规划器的评估主要在nuPlan等基准上进行，但在真实自动驾驶系统中的闭环评估很少。现有方法忽略了ROS 2通信延迟和实时调度约束，且ONNX部署会冻结所有求解器参数，缺乏运行时配置能力。

Method: 使用ONNX GraphSurgeon将包含18,398个节点的单体扩散规划器分解为三个独立可执行模块，在C++中重新实现DPM-Solver++去噪循环。将其作为ROS 2节点集成到Autoware自动驾驶栈中，支持运行时配置求解器参数，并在AWSIM闭环仿真中进行验证。

Result: 编码器缓存使延迟降低3.2倍；二阶求解在N=3时比一阶求解减少FDE 41%。系统在N={3,5,7,10,15,20}的六种步数配置下比较了DPM-Solver++（一阶和二阶）和DDIM的性能。

Conclusion: 该模块化基准测试填补了扩散运动规划器在真实自动驾驶系统中评估的空白，提供了从仿真基准到真实车辆部署的直接路径，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Diffusion-based motion planners have achieved state-of-the-art results on benchmarks such as nuPlan, yet their evaluation within closed-loop production autonomous driving stacks remains largely unexplored. Existing evaluations abstract away ROS 2 communication latency and real-time scheduling constraints, while monolithic ONNX deployment freezes all solver parameters at export time. We present an open-source modular benchmark that addresses both gaps: using ONNX GraphSurgeon, we decompose a monolithic 18,398 node diffusion planner into three independently executable modules and reimplement the DPM-Solver++ denoising loop in native C++. Integrated as a ROS 2 node within Autoware, the open-source AD stack deployed on real vehicles worldwide, the system enables runtime-configurable solver parameters without model recompilation and per-step observability of the denoising process, breaking the black box of monolithic deployment. Unlike evaluations in standalone simulators such as CARLA, our benchmark operates within a production-grade stack and is validated through AWSIM closed-loop simulation. Through systematic comparison of DPM-Solver++ (first- and second-order) and DDIM across six step-count configurations (N in {3, 5, 7, 10, 15, 20}), we show that encoder caching yields a 3.2x latency reduction, and that second-order solving reduces FDE by 41% at N=3 compared to first-order. The complete codebase will be released as open-source, providing a direct path from simulation benchmarks to real-vehicle deployment.

</details>


### [369] [Compact Task-Aligned Imitation Learning for Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01110)
*Kanata Suzuki,Hanon Nakamurama,Kana Miyamoto,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TVF-DiT：一种用于实验室自动化的紧凑型模仿学习框架，使用小型基础模型，参数量少于500M，可在低显存GPU上推理，在三个真实实验室任务中平均成功率86.6%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人实验室自动化依赖精心设计的运动管道和任务特定硬件接口，导致设计成本高、灵活性有限。现有模仿学习技术虽然能生成通用机器人行为，但模型规模大需要高性能计算资源，限制了在实际实验室环境中的应用。

Method: 提出TVF-DiT方法，通过紧凑适配器将自监督视觉基础模型与视觉语言模型对齐，并与基于扩散Transformer的动作专家集成。整个模型参数量少于500M，可在低显存GPU上进行推理。

Result: 在三个真实世界实验室任务（试管清洗、试管排列、粉末转移）上的实验显示平均成功率86.6%，显著优于其他轻量级基线方法。详细的任务提示能改善视觉语言对齐和任务性能。

Conclusion: 小型基础模型经过适当对齐并与基于扩散的策略学习集成后，能够有效支持有限计算资源下的实际实验室自动化应用。

Abstract: Robotic laboratory automation has traditionally relied on carefully engineered motion pipelines and task-specific hardware interfaces, resulting in high design cost and limited flexibility. While recent imitation learning techniques can generate general robot behaviors, their large model sizes often require high-performance computational resources, limiting applicability in practical laboratory environments. In this study, we propose a compact imitation learning framework for laboratory automation using small foundation models. The proposed method, TVF-DiT, aligns a self-supervised vision foundation model with a vision-language model through a compact adapter, and integrates them with a Diffusion Transformer-based action expert. The entire model consists of fewer than 500M parameters, enabling inference on low-VRAM GPUs. Experiments on three real-world laboratory tasks - test tube cleaning, test tube arrangement, and powder transfer - demonstrate an average success rate of 86.6%, significantly outperforming alternative lightweight baselines. Furthermore, detailed task prompts improve vision-language alignment and task performance. These results indicate that small foundation models, when properly aligned and integrated with diffusion-based policy learning, can effectively support practical laboratory automation with limited computational resources.

</details>


### [370] [Fast Confidence-Aware Human Prediction via Hardware-accelerated Bayesian Inference for Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01122)
*Michael Lu,Minh Bui,Xubo Lyu,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于粒子表示的置信感知预测框架，用于机器人对人类行为的长期高频预测，支持125Hz更新率，实现多人在动态环境中的安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人在日常环境中的普及，确保其在人类周围的安全导航变得至关重要。特别是在超市、护理院等受限空间中，机器人需要与多人交互，必须准确预测人类行为以实现高效安全的运动规划。

Method: 提出一种简单而新颖的置信感知预测方法，将未来预测视为粒子。该框架高度并行化并在GPU上加速，支持125Hz的高频更新，可轻松扩展到多人预测，提供更精细的时间步长预测。

Result: 通过真实世界实验验证，机器人能够在具有不同导航目标的多个人类之间安全导航。相比现有方法，支持更精细的预测时间步长，产生更细粒度的轨迹预测，使运动规划器能有效响应人类行为的细微变化。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在动态环境中实现稳健高效人机共存的潜力，通过GPU加速的粒子预测框架实现了长期高频预测，为安全导航提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As robots increasingly integrate into everyday environments, ensuring their safe navigation around humans becomes imperative. Efficient and safe motion planning requires robots to account for human behavior, particularly in constrained spaces such as grocery stores or care homes, where interactions with multiple individuals are common. Prior research has employed Bayesian frameworks to model human rationality based on navigational intent, enabling the prediction of probabilistic trajectories for planning purposes. In this work, we present a simple yet novel approach for confidence-aware prediction that treats future predictions as particles. This framework is highly parallelized and accelerated on an graphics processing unit (GPU). As a result, this enables longer-term predictions at a frequency of 125 Hz and can be easily extended for multi-human predictions. Compared to existing methods, our implementation supports finer prediction time steps, yielding more granular trajectory forecasts. This enhanced resolution allows motion planners to respond effectively to subtle changes in human behavior. We validate our approach through real-world experiments, demonstrating a robot safely navigating among multiple humans with diverse navigational goals. Our results highlight the methods potential for robust and efficient human-robot coexistence in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [371] [Pro-HOI: Perceptive Root-guided Humanoid-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01126)
*Yuhang Lin,Jiyuan Shi,Dewei Wang,Jipeng Kong,Yong Liu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pro-HOI：一种基于感知根引导的人形机器人-物体交互框架，通过根轨迹条件化策略和持续物体估计模块，实现鲁棒的长时程定位操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人执行可靠的HOI任务面临两大挑战：缺乏通用控制接口和鲁棒的闭环感知机制。现有方法需要复杂的奖励调优，且难以实现同时导航和定位操作

Method: 1) 收集适合真实世界部署的箱体搬运运动，通过SDF损失优化穿透伪影；2) 提出新颖训练框架：策略以期望根轨迹为条件，参考运动仅作为奖励；3) 集成持续物体估计模块，融合实时检测与数字孪生技术

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上的实证验证表明，Pro-HOI在泛化性和鲁棒性上显著优于基线方法，能够在复杂真实场景中实现可靠的长时程执行

Conclusion: Pro-HOI通过根轨迹作为通用接口和持续物体估计模块，为人形机器人提供了可泛化、鲁棒的定位操作框架，解决了HOI任务中的关键挑战

Abstract: Executing reliable Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) tasks for humanoid robots is hindered by the lack of generalized control interfaces and robust closed-loop perception mechanisms. In this work, we introduce Perceptive Root-guided Humanoid-Object Interaction, Pro-HOI, a generalizable framework for robust humanoid loco-manipulation. First, we collect box-carrying motions that are suitable for real-world deployment and optimize penetration artifacts through a Signed Distance Field loss. Second, we propose a novel training framework that conditions the policy on a desired root-trajectory while utilizing reference motion exclusively as a reward. This design not only eliminates the need for intricate reward tuning but also establishes root trajectory as a universal interface for high-level planners, enabling simultaneous navigation and loco-manipulation. Furthermore, to ensure operational reliability, we incorporate a persistent object estimation module. By fusing real-time detection with Digital Twin, this module allows the robot to autonomously detect slippage and trigger re-grasping maneuvers. Empirical validation on a Unitree G1 robot demonstrates that Pro-HOI significantly outperforms baselines in generalization and robustness, achieving reliable long-horizon execution in complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [372] [A Deployable Bio-inspired Compliant Leg Design for Enhanced Leaping in Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01128)
*Yiyang Chen,Yuxin Liu,Jinzheng Zhou,Fanxin Wang,Qinglei Bu,Jie Sun,Yikun Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种仿生可展开柔性腿（DCL）设计，通过模拟沫蝉腿部的储能机制，使用3D打印PEBA材料制造轻量化内部晶格结构，显著提升了四足机器人的垂直跳跃能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四足机器人在工业检测和灾难搜救等应用中需要更高的敏捷性和越障能力，但现有平台受限于电机峰值功率不足，无法实现爆发性跳跃。需要解决这一性能限制。

Method: 提出仿生方法模拟沫蝉腿部的储能机制，设计可展开柔性腿（DCL），采用3D打印的聚醚嵌段酰胺（PEBA）弹性材料制造轻量化内部晶格结构，该结构类似生物肌腱，在机器人下蹲阶段储存弹性势能，在跳跃时快速释放以增强电机输出。

Result: 通过有限元分析和实验验证，该机械设计显著提升了机器人的垂直跳跃能力，实现了17.1%的相对性能提升（垂直跳跃高度）。

Conclusion: 仿生可展开柔性腿设计通过模拟生物储能机制，有效克服了电机功率限制，显著增强了四足机器人的爆发性跳跃能力，为需要高敏捷性和越障能力的应用场景提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are becoming increasingly essential for various applications, including industrial inspection and catastrophe search and rescue. These scenarios require robots to possess enhanced agility and obstacle-navigation skills. Nonetheless, the performance of current platforms is often constrained by insufficient peak motor power, limiting their ability to perform explosive jumps. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a bio-inspired method that emulates the energy-storage mechanism found in froghopper legs. We designed a Deployable Compliant Leg (DCL) utilizing a specialized 3D-printed elastic material, Polyether block amide (PEBA), featuring a lightweight internal lattice structure. This structure functions analogously to biological tendons, storing elastic energy during the robot's squatting phase and rapidly releasing it to augment motor output during the leap. The proposed mechanical design significantly enhances the robot's vertical jumping capability. Through finite element analysis (FEA) and experimental validation, we demonstrate a relative performance improvement of 17.1% in vertical jumping height.

</details>


### [373] [RAG-RUSS: A Retrieval-Augmented Robotic Ultrasound for Autonomous Carotid Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01153)
*Dianye Huang,Ziping Cong,Nassir Navab,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAG-RUSS：一种可解释的机器人超声框架，用于颈动脉检查，结合检索增强生成技术提高泛化能力，减少对大规模训练数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声方法存在两个主要问题：1）决策过程通常是基于规则的或依赖于端到端学习的黑盒模型；2）这限制了临床接受度并引发安全担忧。需要一种既能执行完整颈动脉检查又能明确解释当前阶段和下一步计划的可解释框架。

Method: 提出RAG-RUSS框架，结合检索增强生成技术来增强泛化能力并减少对大规模训练数据的依赖。该方法在28名志愿者的数据上进行训练，并在4名未见志愿者的体积扫描数据上进行测试。

Result: 该方法能够解释当前扫描阶段，并自主规划探头运动以完成颈动脉检查，涵盖横断面和纵断面。在未见数据上验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: RAG-RUSS提供了一种可解释的机器人超声解决方案，能够按照临床工作流程执行完整颈动脉检查，同时明确解释决策过程，有望提高临床接受度和安全性。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound (US) has recently attracted increasing attention as a means to overcome the limitations of conventional US examinations, such as the strong operator dependence. However, the decision-making process of existing methods is often either rule-based or relies on end-to-end learning models that operate as black boxes. This has been seen as a main limit for clinical acceptance and raises safety concerns for widespread adoption in routine practice. To tackle this challenge, we introduce the RAG-RUSS, an interpretable framework capable of performing a full carotid examination in accordance with the clinical workflow while explicitly explaining both the current stage and the next planned action. Furthermore, given the scarcity of medical data, we incorporate retrieval-augmented generation to enhance generalization and reduce dependence on large-scale training datasets. The method was trained on data acquired from 28 volunteers, while an additional four volumetric scans recorded from previously unseen volunteers were reserved for testing. The results demonstrate that the method can explain the current scanning stage and autonomously plan probe motions to complete the carotid examination, encompassing both transverse and longitudinal planes.

</details>


### [374] [Path Integral Particle Filtering for Hybrid Systems via Saltation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01176)
*Karthik Shaji,Sreeranj Jayadevan,Bo Yuan,Hongzhe Yu,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于最优控制的粒子滤波方法，用于处理与环境间歇接触的混合系统状态估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合系统与环境间歇接触时的状态估计具有挑战性，需要处理接触动力学、非高斯噪声分布和异常值影响

Method: 采用路径积分滤波框架，利用平滑问题与最优控制的对偶性，使用盐跃矩阵映射接触事件中的不确定性传播

Result: 算法对异常值鲁棒、适应非高斯噪声分布、能处理混合系统的接触动力学，计算高效可靠，在多个设置中持续优于强基线

Conclusion: 为具有随机动力学的混合系统提供了一种计算高效且可靠的状态估计算法

Abstract: We present an optimal-control-based particle filtering method for state estimation in hybrid systems that undergo intermittent contact with their environments. We follow the path integral filtering framework that exploits the duality between the smoothing problem and optimal control. We leverage saltation matrices to map out the uncertainty propagation during contact events for hybrid systems. The resulting path integral optimal control problem allows for a state estimation algorithm robust to outlier effects, flexible to non-Gaussian noise distributions, that also handles the challenging contact dynamics in hybrid systems. This work offers a computationally efficient and reliable estimation algorithm for hybrid systems with stochastic dynamics. We also present extensive experimental results demonstrating that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines across multiple settings.

</details>


### [375] [RMBench: Memory-Dependent Robotic Manipulation Benchmark with Insights into Policy Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01229)
*Tianxing Chen,Yuran Wang,Mingleyang Li,Yan Qin,Hao Shi,Zixuan Li,Yifan Hu,Yingsheng Zhang,Kaixuan Wang,Yue Chen,Hongcheng Wang,Renjing Xu,Ruihai Wu,Yao Mu,Yaodong Yang,Hao Dong,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RMBench基准和Mem-0策略，系统评估机器人操作策略的记忆能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略对记忆能力考虑有限，难以处理需要历史观察推理和时间信息保持的任务，且缺乏系统性的记忆依赖操作评估

Method: 引入RMBench基准（包含9个不同记忆复杂度任务）和Mem-0策略（具有显式记忆模块的模块化设计），支持受控消融研究

Result: 通过仿真和真实实验识别了现有策略的记忆相关限制，提供了架构设计选择如何影响记忆性能的经验见解

Conclusion: RMBench基准和Mem-0策略填补了机器人操作记忆能力系统性评估的空白，为理解架构设计与记忆性能关系提供了实证基础

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies have made rapid progress in recent years, yet most existing approaches give limited consideration to memory capabilities. Consequently, they struggle to solve tasks that require reasoning over historical observations and maintaining task-relevant information over time, which are common requirements in real-world manipulation scenarios. Although several memory-aware policies have been proposed, systematic evaluation of memory-dependent manipulation remains underexplored, and the relationship between architectural design choices and memory performance is still not well understood. To address this gap, we introduce RMBench, a simulation benchmark comprising 9 manipulation tasks that span multiple levels of memory complexity, enabling systematic evaluation of policy memory capabilities. We further propose Mem-0, a modular manipulation policy with explicit memory components designed to support controlled ablation studies. Through extensive simulation and real-world experiments, we identify memory-related limitations in existing policies and provide empirical insights into how architectural design choices influence memory performance. The website is available at https://rmbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [376] [Certifiable Estimation with Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01267)
*Zhexin Xu,Nikolas R. Sanderson,Hanna Jiamei Zhang,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将因子图优化与可认证估计统一的新框架，通过保留Shor松弛和Burer-Monteiro分解下的因子图结构，使得可认证估计能够直接利用现有的成熟因子图库和工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因子图优化使用局部优化方法可能收敛到次优解，这在安全关键应用中存在可靠性问题；而基于凸松弛的可认证估计器虽然能恢复可验证的全局最优解，但其大规模松弛求解的计算成本需要专门的、利用结构的求解器，实现难度大，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 提出将因子图优化与可认证估计统一的方法：关键洞察是因子图结构在Shor松弛和Burer-Monteiro分解下得以保留。将这两种变换应用于具有关联因子图表示的QCQP，会得到一个提升问题，该问题具有相同连接性的因子图模型，其中变量和因子是原始QCQP中对应元素的一对一代数变换。

Result: 这种结构保留使得Riemannian Staircase方法能够使用与常规因子图推理相同的成熟、高性能因子图库和工作流程来实现可认证估计，使可认证估计的设计和部署变得与常规因子图推理一样简单。

Conclusion: 该论文成功地将因子图优化和可认证估计这两个在文献中被独立处理的范式统一起来，为可认证因子图优化提供了一个统一的框架，显著降低了可认证估计在实际机器人应用中的部署门槛。

Abstract: Factor graphs provide a convenient modular modeling language that enables practitioners to design and deploy high-performance robotic state estimation systems by composing simple, reusable building blocks. However, inference in these models is typically performed using local optimization methods that can converge to suboptimal solutions, a serious reliability concern in safety-critical applications. Conversely, certifiable estimators based on convex relaxation can recover verifiably globally optimal solutions in many practical settings, but the computational cost of solving their large-scale relaxations necessitates specialized, structure-exploiting solvers that require substantial expertise to implement, significantly hampering practical deployment.
