  In this paper, we show that these two paradigms, which have thus far been treated as independent in the literature, can be naturally synthesized into a unified framework for certifiable factor graph optimization. The key insight is that factor graph structure is preserved under Shor's relaxation and Burer-Monteiro factorization: applying these transformations to a QCQP with an associated factor graph representation yields a lifted problem admitting a factor graph model with identical connectivity, in which variables and factors are simple one-to-one algebraic transformations of those in the original QCQP. This structural preservation enables the Riemannian Staircase methodology for certifiable estimation to be implemented using the same mature, highly-performant factor graph libraries and workflows already ubiquitously employed throughout robotics and computer vision, making certifiable estimation as straightforward to design and deploy as conventional factor graph inference.

</details>


### [377] [Spherical Latent Motion Prior for Physics-Based Simulated Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01294)
*Jing Tan,Weisheng Xu,Xiangrui Jiang,Jiaxi Zhang,Kun Yang,Kai Wu,Jiaqi Xiong,Shiting Chen,Yangfan Li,Yixiao Feng,Yuetong Fang,Yujia Zou,Yiqun Song,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLMP是一种两阶段方法，通过高质量运动跟踪控制器和球形潜在空间蒸馏，解决了VAE信息损失和AMP模式崩溃问题，实现了无信息损失、稳定随机采样的运动先验学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动先验学习方法存在明显缺陷：VAE存在信息损失，随机潜在采样可能产生无效行为；AMP存在模式崩溃问题，难以捕捉多样化的运动技能。需要一种既能保留精细运动细节，又能实现稳定随机采样的方法。

Method: SLMP采用两阶段方法：第一阶段训练高质量运动跟踪控制器；第二阶段将跟踪控制器蒸馏到球形潜在空间。结合蒸馏、判别器和判别器引导的局部语义一致性约束，形成结构化的潜在动作空间，实现无信息损失的稳定随机采样。

Result: SLMP在收集的两小时人类战斗动作捕捉数据集上验证，能够保留精细运动细节且无信息损失，随机采样产生语义有效且稳定的行为。应用于双智能体物理战斗任务时，仅使用简单基于规则的奖励就能产生类人且物理合理的战斗行为。此外，SLMP能够泛化到不同人形机器人形态，展示了其可迁移性。

Conclusion: SLMP通过球形潜在运动先验方法，有效解决了现有VAE和AMP方法的局限性，实现了高质量、多样化、可泛化的运动先验学习，为物理人形控制提供了更优的运动先验解决方案。

Abstract: Learning motion priors for physics-based humanoid control is an active research topic. Existing approaches mainly include variational autoencoders (VAE) and adversarial motion priors (AMP). VAE introduces information loss, and random latent sampling may sometimes produce invalid behaviors. AMP suffers from mode collapse and struggles to capture diverse motion skills. We present the Spherical Latent Motion Prior (SLMP), a two-stage method for learning motion priors. In the first stage, we train a high-quality motion tracking controller. In the second stage, we distill the tracking controller into a spherical latent space. A combination of distillation, a discriminator, and a discriminator-guided local semantic consistency constraint shapes a structured latent action space, allowing stable random sampling without information loss. To evaluate SLMP, we collect a two-hour human combat motion capture dataset and show that SLMP preserves fine motion detail without information loss, and random sampling yields semantically valid and stable behaviors. When applied to a two-agent physics-based combat task, SLMP produces human-like and physically plausible combat behaviors only using simple rule-based rewards. Furthermore, SLMP generalizes across different humanoid robot morphologies, demonstrating its transferability beyond a single simulated avatar.

</details>


### [378] [Hybrid TD3: Overestimation Bias Analysis and Stable Policy Optimization for Hybrid Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01302)
*Thanh-Tuan Tran,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Nak Young Chong,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid TD3算法，扩展TD3以原生处理参数化混合动作空间，通过理论分析过估计偏差并引入加权裁剪Q学习目标，在机器人操作任务中实现更好的训练稳定性和性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的强化学习面临离散-连续混合动作空间的基本挑战，现有方法要么离散化连续分量，要么将离散选择松弛为连续近似，在高维动作空间和领域随机化下存在可扩展性限制和训练不稳定问题

Method: 提出Hybrid TD3算法，扩展TD3以原生处理参数化混合动作空间；进行混合动作设置下过估计偏差的严格理论分析，推导双评论家架构下的形式化边界；引入加权裁剪Q学习目标，通过对离散动作分布的边缘化实现偏差减少

Result: Hybrid TD3在机器人操作任务中实现了优越的训练稳定性和与最先进混合动作基线相比的竞争性性能

Conclusion: Hybrid TD3通过理论驱动的偏差分析和加权裁剪Q学习目标，为混合动作空间强化学习提供了稳定高效的解决方案，在机器人操作任务中表现出色

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in discrete-continuous hybrid action spaces presents fundamental challenges for robotic manipulation, where high-level task decisions and low-level joint-space execution must be jointly optimized. Existing approaches either discretize continuous components or relax discrete choices into continuous approximations, which suffer from scalability limitations and training instability in high-dimensional action spaces and under domain randomization. In this paper, we propose Hybrid TD3, an extension of Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) that natively handles parameterized hybrid action spaces in a principled manner. We conduct a rigorous theoretical analysis of overestimation bias in hybrid action settings, deriving formal bounds under twin-critic architectures and establishing a complete bias ordering across five algorithmic variants. Building on this analysis, we introduce a weighted clipped Q-learning target that marginalizes over the discrete action distribution, achieving equivalent bias reduction to standard clipped minimization while improving policy smoothness. Experimental results demonstrate that Hybrid TD3 achieves superior training stability and competitive performance against state-of-the-art hybrid action baselines

</details>


### [379] [D-GVIO: A Buffer-Driven and Efficient Decentralized GNSS-Visual-Inertial State Estimator for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01404)
*Yarong Luo,Wentao Lu,Chi Guo,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-GVIO：一种基于缓冲策略的完全去中心化GNSS-视觉-惯性里程计框架，通过协方差分割、协方差交集、左不变扩展卡尔曼滤波和自适应异常检测，实现高效、鲁棒的分布式状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体应用（如协作探索、搜救任务）需要实时、鲁棒且计算高效的协同定位，但在资源受限平台上实现这些特性面临重大挑战。现有方法在计算负担、通信开销和GNSS受限环境下的鲁棒性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出D-GVIO框架，包含四个核心机制：1）通过协方差分割、协方差交集和缓冲策略模块化分布式状态估计的传播和更新步骤；2）采用左不变扩展卡尔曼滤波进行信息融合；3）基于缓冲的重传播策略处理延迟测量；4）自适应缓冲驱动异常检测方法动态剔除GNSS异常值。

Result: 显著降低了计算和通信负担，提升了状态估计性能，能够高效准确地处理延迟测量，并在GNSS受限环境中增强了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: D-GVIO通过创新的缓冲策略和左不变滤波方法，为资源受限平台上的群体应用提供了一种高效、鲁棒且完全去中心化的协同定位解决方案。

Abstract: Cooperative localization is essential for swarm applications like collaborative exploration and search-and-rescue missions. However, maintaining real-time capability, robustness, and computational efficiency on resource-constrained platforms presents significant challenges. To address these challenges, we propose D-GVIO, a buffer-driven and fully decentralized GNSS-Visual-Inertial Odometry (GVIO) framework that leverages a novel buffering strategy to support efficient and robust distributed state estimation. The proposed framework is characterized by four core mechanisms. Firstly, through covariance segmentation, covariance intersection and buffering strategy, we modularize propagation and update steps in distributed state estimation, significantly reducing computational and communication burdens. Secondly, the left-invariant extended Kalman filter (L-IEKF) is adopted for information fusion, which exhibits superior state estimation performance over the traditional extended Kalman filter (EKF) since its state transition matrix is independent of the system state. Thirdly, a buffer-based re-propagation strategy is employed to handle delayed measurements efficiently and accurately by leveraging the L-IEKF, eliminating the need for costly re-computation. Finally, an adaptive buffer-driven outlier detection method is proposed to dynamically cull GNSS outliers, enhancing robustness in GNSS-challenged environments.

</details>


### [380] [PhysGraph: Physically-Grounded Graph-Transformer Policies for Bimanual Dexterous Hand-Tool-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01436)
*Runfa Blark Li,David Kim,Xinshuang Liu,Keito Suzuki,Dwait Bhatt,Nikola Raicevic,Xin Lin,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysGraph：基于物理图变换器的双手机器人工具操作策略，通过图表示和物理先验提升操作精度和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人工具操作面临高维状态空间和复杂接触动力学的挑战。现有方法将整个系统状态表示为单一配置向量，忽略了关节手的丰富结构和拓扑信息，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出PhysGraph物理图变换器策略：1）将双手机器人系统表示为运动学图；2）引入每连杆标记化以保留细粒度局部状态信息；3）设计物理基础偏置生成器，将运动学空间距离、动态接触状态、几何接近度和解剖特性等结构先验直接注入注意力机制。

Result: PhysGraph在操作精度和任务成功率上显著优于基线方法ManipTrans，同时仅使用ManipTrans 51%的参数。此外，架构的拓扑灵活性实现了对未见工具/物体几何的零样本迁移，并能在三种机器人手（Shadow、Allegro、Inspire）上训练。

Conclusion: PhysGraph通过图表示和物理先验注入，使策略能够显式推理物理交互而非从稀疏奖励中隐式学习，为双手机器人工具操作提供了高效且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation for tool use remains a formidable challenge in robotics due to the high-dimensional state space and complicated contact dynamics. Existing methods naively represent the entire system state as a single configuration vector, disregarding the rich structural and topological information inherent to articulated hands. We present PhysGraph, a physically-grounded graph transformer policy designed explicitly for challenging bimanual hand-tool-object manipulation. Unlike prior works, we represent the bimanual system as a kinematic graph and introduce per-link tokenization to preserve fine-grained local state information. We propose a physically-grounded bias generator that injects structural priors directly into the attention mechanism, including kinematic spatial distance, dynamic contact states, geometric proximity, and anatomical properties. This allows the policy to explicitly reason about physical interactions rather than learning them implicitly from sparse rewards. Extensive experiments show that PhysGraph significantly outperforms baseline - ManipTrans in manipulation precision and task success rates while using only 51% of the parameters of ManipTrans. Furthermore, the inherent topological flexibility of our architecture shows qualitative zero-shot transfer to unseen tool/object geometries, and is sufficiently general to be trained on three robotic hands (Shadow, Allegro, Inspire).

</details>


### [381] [Non-Markovian Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Keyframe Chaining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01465)
*Yipeng Chen,Wentao Tan,Lei Zhu,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Guoli Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Keyframe-Chaining VLA框架，通过提取和链接关键历史帧来建模长时程依赖，解决现有VLA模型在非马尔可夫任务中的泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型在处理长时程任务时过度依赖即时观测，难以捕捉非马尔可夫依赖关系（最优动作仅依赖于特定历史状态而非当前观测）。需要一种能够显式建模长时程时间依赖的方法。

Method: 1. 自动关键帧选择器：学习判别性嵌入空间，识别不同的状态转换；2. 进度感知查询机制：基于当前执行阶段的时间相关性动态检索历史帧；3. 关键帧作为交错视觉令牌集成到VLA中，显式地将策略锚定在长时程时间上下文中。

Result: 在基于ManiSkill模拟器构建的四个非马尔可夫操作任务上进行了实验，结果显示该方法取得了优越的性能，有效解决了具有长时程时间依赖的机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: Keyframe-Chaining VLA框架通过显式建模长时程依赖关系，显著提升了VLA模型在非马尔可夫任务中的性能，为处理复杂机器人操作任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often struggle to generalize to long-horizon tasks due to their heavy reliance on immediate observations. While recent studies incorporate retrieval mechanisms or extend context windows to handle procedural tasks, they often struggle to capture Non-Markovian dependencies, where optimal actions rely solely on specific past states rather than the current observation. To address this, we introduce Keyframe-Chaining VLA, a framework that extracts and links key historical frames to model long-horizon dependencies. Specifically, we propose an automatic keyframe selector that learns a discriminative embedding space, effectively identifying distinct state transitions. To capture task-critical information, we design a progress-aware query mechanism that dynamically retrieves historical frames based on their temporal relevance to the current execution phase. These selected keyframes are integrated into the VLA as interleaved visual tokens, explicitly grounding the policy in the long-horizon temporal context. Finally, we introduce a suite of four Non-Markovian manipulation tasks built upon the ManiSkill simulator to measure task success rates. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance, effectively tackling robot manipulation tasks characterized by long-horizon temporal dependencies. Code is available at https://github.com/cytoplastm/KC-VLA.

</details>


### [382] [Mean-Flow based One-Step Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01469)
*Yang Chen,Xiaoguang Ma,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Mean-Flow的单步VLA方法，解决FlowMatching框架生成延迟问题，实现高效动作生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FlowMatching-based VLA框架虽然在灵巧机器人操作任务中表现出色，但存在生成延迟问题，主要源于迭代采样需求和架构限制，限制了实际应用

Method: 提出Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA方法，解决动作生成过程中的噪声诱导问题，消除传统Flow-Matching方法的一致性约束，实现单步动作生成

Result: 真实机器人实验显示，所提方法的生成速度比SmolVLA快8.7倍，比Diffusion Policy快83.9倍，显著提升生成效率

Conclusion: 该方法具有作为VLA机器人操作高效骨干的巨大潜力，解决了现有方法的延迟瓶颈问题

Abstract: Recent advances in FlowMatching-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks have demonstrated remarkable advantages in generating high-frequency action chunks, particularly for highly dexterous robotic manipulation tasks. Despite these notable achievements, their practical applications are constrained by prolonged generation latency, which stems from inherent iterative sampling requirements and architectural limitations. To address this critical bottleneck, we propose a Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA approach. Specifically, we resolve the noise-induced issues in the action generation process, thereby eliminating the consistency constraints inherent to conventional Flow-Matching methods. This significantly enhances generation efficiency and enables one-step action generation. Real-world robotic experiments show that the generation speed of the proposed Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA is 8.7 times and 83.9 times faster than that of SmolVLA and Diffusion Policy, respectively. These results elucidate its great potential as a high-efficiency backbone for VLA-based robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [383] [ROSER: Few-Shot Robotic Sequence Retrieval for Scalable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01474)
*Zillur Rahman,Eddison Pham,Alejandro Daniel Noel,Cristian Meo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSER：一种轻量级少样本检索框架，从无标签的机器人交互日志中提取可重用的任务中心轨迹片段，仅需3-5个参考示例，无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习面临任务标记、分段训练数据稀缺的瓶颈，尽管存在大量作为长连续交互日志记录的大规模机器人数据集。现有数据集包含大量多样化行为，但在结构上与需要干净分段、任务特定轨迹的现代学习框架不兼容。

Method: 提出机器人序列检索任务，从无标签日志中提取可重用任务中心片段。引入ROSER框架，学习时间窗口上的任务无关度量空间，仅需3-5个演示示例即可实现准确检索，无需任何任务特定训练。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集（LIBERO、DROID、nuScenes）上评估，ROSER在准确性和效率上均优于传统对齐方法、学习嵌入和语言模型基线，实现每匹配亚毫秒级推理，同时保持优越的分布对齐。

Conclusion: 通过将数据管理重构为少样本检索，ROSER为解锁未充分利用的机器人数据集提供了实用途径，从根本上改善了机器人学习的数据可用性。

Abstract: A critical bottleneck in robot learning is the scarcity of task-labeled, segmented training data, despite the abundance of large-scale robotic datasets recorded as long, continuous interaction logs. Existing datasets contain vast amounts of diverse behaviors, yet remain structurally incompatible with modern learning frameworks that require cleanly segmented, task-specific trajectories. We address this data utilization crisis by formalizing robotic sequence retrieval: the task of extracting reusable, task-centric segments from unlabeled logs using only a few reference examples. We introduce ROSER, a lightweight few-shot retrieval framework that learns task-agnostic metric spaces over temporal windows, enabling accurate retrieval with as few as 3-5 demonstrations, without any task-specific training required. To validate our approach, we establish comprehensive evaluation protocols and benchmark ROSER against classical alignment methods, learned embeddings, and language model baselines across three large-scale datasets (e.g., LIBERO, DROID, and nuScenes). Our experiments demonstrate that ROSER consistently outperforms all prior methods in both accuracy and efficiency, achieving sub-millisecond per-match inference while maintaining superior distributional alignment. By reframing data curation as few-shot retrieval, ROSER provides a practical pathway to unlock underutilized robotic datasets, fundamentally improving data availability for robot learning.

</details>


### [384] [SFCo-Nav: Efficient Zero-Shot Visual Language Navigation via Collaboration of Slow LLM and Fast Attributed Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01477)
*Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Kehui Ma,Ziyi Xia,Zixin Jiang,Zhen Sun,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SFCo-Nav是一个基于慢-快认知协作原理的高效零样本视觉语言导航框架，通过慢LLM规划器、快反应导航器和异步慢-快桥接模块，在保持导航成功率的同时显著降低计算成本和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本VLN方法通常构建简单的观察图并进行每步VLM-LLM推理，导致高延迟和高计算成本，限制了实时部署。需要一种更高效的零样本导航框架来降低计算开销同时保持导航性能。

Method: SFCo-Nav采用慢-快认知协作架构，包含三个关键模块：1) 慢LLM规划器生成战略性子目标链，每个子目标关联想象的物体图；2) 快反应导航器实时构建物体图并执行子目标；3) 轻量级异步慢-快桥接模块对齐结构化、带属性的想象图和感知图来估计导航置信度，仅在必要时触发慢LLM规划器。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE基准测试中，SFCo-Nav达到或超过先前最先进零样本VLN方法的成功率，同时将每条轨迹的总token消耗减少50%以上，运行速度提升3.5倍以上。在酒店套房的腿式机器人上展示了其室内环境中的效率和实用性。

Conclusion: SFCo-Nav是首个支持根据内部置信度异步触发LLM的慢-快协作零样本VLN系统，通过智能的慢-快模块协作显著提高了计算效率，为实时视觉语言导航提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (VLMs) and large language models (LLMs) have enabled zero-shot approaches to visual language navigation (VLN), where an agent follows natural language instructions using only ego perception and reasoning. However, existing zero-shot methods typically construct a naive observation graph and perform per-step VLM-LLM inference on it, resulting in high latency and computation costs that limit real-time deployment. To address this, we present SFCo-Nav, an efficient zero-shot VLN framework inspired by the principle of slow-fast cognitive collaboration. SFCo-Nav integrates three key modules: 1) a slow LLM-based planner that produces a strategic chain of subgoals, each linked to an imagined object graph; 2) a fast reactive navigator for real-time object graph construction and subgoal execution; and 3) a lightweight asynchronous slow-fast bridge aligns advanced structured, attributed imagined and perceived graphs to estimate navigation confidence, triggering the slow LLM planner only when necessary. To the best of our knowledge, SFCo-Nav is the first slow-fast collaboration zero-shot VLN system supporting asynchronous LLM triggering according to the internal confidence. Evaluated on the public R2R and REVERIE benchmarks, SFCo-Nav matches or exceeds prior state-of-the-art zero-shot VLN success rates while cutting total token consumption per trajectory by over 50% and running more than 3.5 times faster. Finally, we demonstrate SFCo-Nav on a legged robot in a hotel suite, showcasing its efficiency and practicality in indoor environments.

</details>


### [385] [Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy for Safe Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01479)
*Kunlin Xie Chenghao Li Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAQP框架通过异构双补丁优化方案和梯度级模态平衡策略，实现多模态安全抓取，解决RGBD模态中对抗攻击的有效性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于DNN的视觉引导机器人抓取在HRI中存在安全风险，现有RGB模态对抗攻击方法在RGBD模态中效果有限，需要解决多模态下的安全抓取问题。

Method: 提出MAQP框架：1) HDPOS采用模态特定初始化策略（深度用高斯分布、RGB用均匀分布），在统一目标函数下联合优化；2) GLMBS基于通道敏感性分析重新加权梯度贡献，应用距离自适应扰动边界解决优化不平衡。

Result: 在基准数据集和协作机器人上进行了广泛实验，证明了MAQP的有效性。

Conclusion: MAQP通过解决RGB和深度模态间的分布差异和优化不平衡问题，实现了有效的多模态安全抓取。

Abstract: Vision-guided robot grasping based on Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) generalizes well but poses safety risks in the Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). Recent works solved it by designing benign adversarial attacks and patches with RGB modality, yet depth-independent characteristics limit their effectiveness on RGBD modality. In this work, we propose the Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy (MAQP) to realize multimodal safe grasping. Our framework introduces two key components. First, the Heterogeneous Dual-Patch Optimization Scheme (HDPOS) mitigates the distribution discrepancy between RGB and depth modalities in patch generation by adopting modality-specific initialization strategies, employing a Gaussian distribution for depth patches and a uniform distribution for RGB patches, while jointly optimizing both modalities under a unified objective function. Second, the Gradient-Level Modality Balancing Strategy (GLMBS) is designed to resolve the optimization imbalance from RGB and Depth patches in patch shape adaptation by reweighting gradient contributions based on per-channel sensitivity analysis and applying distance-adaptive perturbation bounds. We conduct extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets and a cobot, showing the effectiveness of MAQP.

</details>


### [386] [FATE: Closed-Loop Feasibility-Aware Task Generation with Active Repair for Physically Grounded Robotic Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01505)
*Bingchuan Wei,Bingqi Huang,Jingheng Ma,Zeyu zhang,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FATE是一个可行性感知的任务生成框架，通过将通用具身智能体嵌入生成循环，实现闭环自校正的任务课程生成，确保物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的生成模拟方法常产生语言连贯但物理不可行的目标，主要原因是任务规范未接地或目标公式未对齐，需要解决这一关键限制。

Method: FATE采用闭环自校正框架，将任务生成重构为迭代验证和精炼过程。包含顺序审计流程：先验证静态场景属性（对象可供性、布局兼容性），再通过模拟具身交互验证执行可行性。检测到不可行任务时，部署主动修复模块自适应调整场景配置或策略规范。

Result: 实验验证FATE能生成语义多样且物理接地的任务课程，相对于最先进的生成基线，在执行失败率方面实现了显著降低。

Conclusion: FATE通过将通用具身智能体直接嵌入生成循环，主动保证生成课程物理接地性，解决了开放环生成方法中物理不可行任务的问题，实现了可行性感知的任务生成。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in generative simulation have harnessed Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate diverse robotic task curricula, yet these open-loop paradigms frequently produce linguistically coherent but physically infeasible goals, stemming from ungrounded task specifications or misaligned objective formulations. To address this critical limitation, we propose FATE (Feasibility-Aware Task gEneration), a closed-loop, self-correcting framework that reimagines task generation as an iterative validation-and-refinement process. Unlike conventional methods that decouple generation and verification into discrete stages, FATE embeds a generalist embodied agent directly into the generation loop to proactively guarantee the physical groundedness of the resulting curriculum. FATE instantiates a sequential auditing pipeline: it first validates static scene attributes (e.g., object affordances, layout compatibility) and subsequently verifies execution feasibility via simulated embodied interaction. Critical to its performance, upon detecting an infeasible task, FATE deploys an active repair module that autonomously adapts scene configurations or policy specifications, converting unworkable proposals into physically valid task instances. Extensive experiments validate that FATE generates semantically diverse, physically grounded task curricula while achieving a substantial reduction in execution failure rates relative to state-of-the-art generative baselines.

</details>


### [387] [KERV: Kinematic-Rectified Speculative Decoding for Embodied VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01581)
*Zihao Zheng,Zhihao Mao,Maoliang Li,Jiayu Chen,Xinhao Sun,Zhaobo Zhang,Donggang Cao,Hong Mei,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KERV框架将视觉-语言-动作模型的令牌域预测与运动学域预测结合，通过卡尔曼滤波补偿推测解码错误，实现27%~37%的加速且几乎不影响成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型采用令牌域控制但速度慢，推测解码可加速但存在两个关键问题：1) 令牌错误需要重新推理计算成本高；2) 接受阈值需要精细调整。同时现有具身智能忽视了机器人运动学的应用。

Method: 提出KERV框架，创新性地结合令牌域VLA模型与运动学域预测进行推测解码。使用基于运动学的卡尔曼滤波器预测动作并补偿SD错误，避免昂贵的重新推理。设计基于运动学的调整策略动态修正接受阈值。

Result: 在多样化任务和环境中的实验结果表明，KERV实现了27%~37%的加速，同时几乎不损失成功率。

Conclusion: KERV框架通过将令牌域VLA模型与运动学域预测相结合，有效解决了推测解码在机器人控制中的两个关键问题，实现了显著的速度提升且保持性能稳定。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build a token-domain robot control paradigm, yet suffer from low speed. Speculative Decoding (SD) is an optimization strategy that can boost inference speed. Two key issues emerge when integrating VLA and SD: first, SD relies on re-inference to address token errors, which is computationally expensive; second, to mitigate token errors, the acceptance threshold in SD requires careful adjustment. Existing works fail to address the above two issues effectively. Meanwhile, as the bridge between AI and the physical world, existing embodied intelligence has overlooked the application of robotic kinematics. To address these issues, we innovatively combine token-domain VLA models with kinematic-domain prediction for SD, proposing a kinematic-rectified SD framework named KERV. We employ a kinematics-based Kalman Filter to predict actions and compensate for SD errors, avoiding costly re-inference. Moreover, we design a kinematics-based adjustment strategy to dynamically rectify the acceptance threshold, addressing the difficulty of threshold determination. Experimental results across diverse tasks and environments demonstrate that KERV achieves 27%~37% acceleration with nearly no Success Rate loss.

</details>


### [388] [Learning Thermal-Aware Locomotion Policies for an Electrically-Actuated Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01631)
*Letian Qian,Yuhang Wan,Shuhan Wang,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出热感知控制方法，将电机温度纳入强化学习运动策略，防止过热保护触发，延长四足机器人持续工作时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动四足机器人在复杂地形上具有高机动性，但其电机在高扭矩循环负载下容易积累热量，可能触发过热保护，限制了长时间任务执行能力

Method: 提出热感知控制方法，将电机温度纳入强化学习运动策略，引入热约束奖励函数防止温度超限

Result: 在Unitree A1机器人上实验，固定3kg负载下，基线策略约7分钟触发过热保护停止，而所提方法可连续运行超过27分钟无热中断，同时保持相当的指令跟踪性能

Conclusion: 热感知控制方法显著提升了四足机器人的可持续运行能力，解决了电机过热限制长时间任务的问题

Abstract: Electrically-actuated quadrupedal robots possess high mobility on complex terrains, but their motors tend to accumulate heat under high-torque cyclic loads, potentially triggering overheat protection and limiting long-duration tasks. This work proposes a thermal-aware control method that incorporates motor temperatures into reinforcement learning locomotion policies and introduces thermal-constraint rewards to prevent temperature exceedance. Real-world experiments on the Unitree A1 demonstrate that, under a fixed 3 kg payload, the baseline policy triggers overheat protection and stops within approximately 7 minutes, whereas the proposed method can operate continuously for over 27 minutes without thermal interruptions while maintaining comparable command-tracking performance, thereby enhancing sustainable operational capability.

</details>


### [389] [B$^2$F-Map: Crowd-sourced Mapping with Bayesian B-spline Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01673)
*Yiping Xie,Yuxuan Xia,Erik Stenborg,Junsheng Fu,Axel Beauvisage,Gabriel E. Garcia,Tianyu Wu,Gustaf Hendeby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: B²F-Map：一种基于众包车辆数据的完整高精地图生成流程，使用单目相机、消费级GNSS和IMU，通过贝叶斯B样条融合处理不确定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有众包地图生成方法要么依赖先验高精地图，要么忽视地图融合中的不确定性。需要一种仅使用低成本传感器（单目相机、消费级GNSS、IMU）就能生成高精地图的完整解决方案，并能有效处理不确定性。

Method: 提出完整的三阶段流程：1）基于轻量级标准地图的云端定位；2）基于扩展对象轨迹PMB滤波器和Gibbs采样的车载建图；3）云端多行程优化和贝叶斯地图融合。使用B样条表示车道线，每个B样条由高斯分布控制点序列参数化，并提出新颖的贝叶斯融合框架处理不同密度表示的B样条轨迹。

Result: 在多种驾驶条件下的大规模真实世界数据集上评估B²F-Map方法，证明该方法能够生成几何一致的车道级地图。

Conclusion: B²F-Map提供了一种完整的低成本高精地图生成解决方案，通过贝叶斯融合框架有效处理不确定性，实现了仅使用消费级传感器的众包地图生成。

Abstract: Crowd-sourced mapping offers a scalable alternative to creating maps using traditional survey vehicles. Yet, existing methods either rely on prior high-definition (HD) maps or neglect uncertainties in the map fusion. In this work, we present a complete pipeline for HD map generation using production vehicles equipped only with a monocular camera, consumer-grade GNSS, and IMU. Our approach includes on-cloud localization using lightweight standard-definition maps, on-vehicle mapping via an extended object trajectory (EOT) Poisson multi-Bernoulli (PMB) filter with Gibbs sampling, and on-cloud multi-drive optimization and Bayesian map fusion. We represent the lane lines using B-splines, where each B-spline is parameterized by a sequence of Gaussian distributed control points, and propose a novel Bayesian fusion framework for B-spline trajectories with differing density representation, enabling principled handling of uncertainties. We evaluate our proposed approach, B$^2$F-Map, on large-scale real-world datasets collected across diverse driving conditions and demonstrate that our method is able to produce geometrically consistent lane-level maps.

</details>


### [390] [TacMamba: A Tactile History Compression Adapter Bridging Fast Reflexes and Slow VLA Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01700)
*Zhenan Wang,Yanzhe Wang,Meixuan Ren,Peng Li,Yang Liu,Yifei Nie,Limin Long,Yun Ye,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhen Zhu,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacMamba：一种分层架构，通过Mamba-based Tactile History Compressor将高频触觉反射与低频视觉规划对齐，解决触觉与视觉融合中的时空不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉模糊的操作任务中，触觉反馈常是唯一真实来源，但触觉与视觉融合面临时空不匹配挑战：触觉感知需要高频处理与长时记忆（系统1），而视觉策略在低频控制频率下运行（系统2）。现有架构难以弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出TacMamba分层架构，包含三个核心贡献：1）定制高频触觉接口；2）基于Mamba的触觉历史压缩器，以O(1)推理延迟（0.45ms）编码连续力历史为紧凑状态，实现与VLA模型的即插即用融合；3）触觉引导的双阶段训练策略，利用时间判别进行自监督表示学习，并通过相位均匀采样缓解数据稀疏性。

Result: 在离散计数和隐式状态切换实验中，TacMamba达到100%成功率，显著优于仅视觉的pi_0.5基线，同时严格满足硬实时约束。

Conclusion: TacMamba成功解决了触觉与视觉融合的时空不匹配问题，通过分层架构实现了高频触觉反射与低频视觉规划的有效对齐，为触觉增强的视觉语言动作模型提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: In visually ambiguous manipulation such as detecting button click tactile feedback is often the sole source of ground truth. However, fusing tactile data poses a significant challenge due to a spatiotemporal mismatch: tactile perception requires high-frequency processing with long-horizon memory (System 1), whereas visual policies operate at low control frequencies (System 2). Existing architectures struggle to bridge this gap: Transformers are computationally prohibitive for high-frequency loops (>100Hz), while LSTMs suffer from forgetting over extended interaction histories. In this paper, we introduce TacMamba, a hierarchical architecture that aligns high-bandwidth tactile reflexes with low-frequency visual planning. Our approach comprises three core contributions: (1) a custom high-frequency tactile interface designed for flexible integration; (2) a Mamba-based Tactile History Compressor that encodes continuous force history into a compact state with O(1) inference latency (0.45 ms), enabling plug-and-play fusion with VLA models without joint pre-training and (3) a Tactile-Guided Dual-Stage Training strategy that leverages temporal discrimination for self-supervised representation learning and phase-uniform sampling to mitigate data sparsity. Experiments on discrete counting and implicit state switching demonstrate that TacMamba achieves 100% success rates, significantly outperforming the visual-only pi_0.5 baseline, while strictly satisfying hard real-time constraints.

</details>


### [391] [A Safety-Aware Shared Autonomy Framework with BarrierIK Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01705)
*Berk Guler,Kay Pompetzki,Yuanzheng Sun,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在共享自主系统中，通过控制屏障函数在逆运动学层实现安全过滤，确保混合后的指令在杂乱环境中保持碰撞安全，同时维持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，线性混合操作者意图和自主辅助可能产生不安全指令，即使各自单独是安全的。现有方法通过势能或成本项建模避障，只能作为软约束，缺乏硬安全保证。

Method: 在共享自主系统的逆运动学层应用控制屏障函数，设计安全过滤器，确保混合后的指令满足硬安全约束，同时保持任务性能。

Result: 仿真和VR远程操作研究表明：在逆运动学层使用CBF减少了违规时间，增加了最小间隙，同时保持了任务性能。用户报告感知安全性、信任度更高，干扰更低，更偏好带安全过滤器的共享自主系统。

Conclusion: 在共享自主系统的逆运动学层集成控制屏障函数能有效提供硬安全保证，改善用户感知安全性和信任度，同时维持任务性能，是提高共享自主系统安全性的有效方法。

Abstract: Shared autonomy blends operator intent with autonomous assistance. In cluttered environments, linear blending can produce unsafe commands even when each source is individually collision-free. Many existing approaches model obstacle avoidance through potentials or cost terms, which only enforce safety as a soft constraint. In contrast, safety-critical control requires hard guarantees. We investigate the use of control barrier functions (CBFs) at the inverse kinematics (IK) layer of shared autonomy, targeting post-blend safety while preserving task performance. Our approach is evaluated in simulation on representative cluttered environments and in a VR teleoperation study comparing pure teleoperation with shared autonomy. Across conditions, employing CBFs at the IK layer reduces violation time and increases minimum clearance while maintaining task performance. In the user study, participants reported higher perceived safety and trust, lower interference, and an overall preference for shared autonomy with our safety filter. Additional materials available at https://berkguler.github.io/barrierik.

</details>


### [392] [Shape-Interpretable Visual Self-Modeling Enables Geometry-Aware Continuum Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01751)
*Peng Yu,Xin Wang,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝塞尔曲线表示和神经ODE的连续体机器人形状可解释视觉自建模框架，实现几何感知控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人具有高灵活性和冗余性，适合复杂环境中的安全交互，但其连续变形和非线性动力学给感知、建模和控制带来了根本性挑战。现有基于视觉的控制方法通常依赖端到端学习，缺乏对机器人几何形状及其与环境交互的显式认知。

Method: 1) 使用贝塞尔曲线表示从多视角平面图像编码机器人形状，将视觉观测转换为紧凑且物理意义明确的形状空间；2) 基于此表示，采用神经常微分方程从数据中自建模形状和末端执行器动力学；3) 实现无需解析模型或密集身体标记的混合形状-位置控制。

Result: 在电缆驱动连续体机器人上的实验表明：形状误差在图像分辨率的1.56%以内，末端执行器误差在机器人长度的2%以内；在受限环境中表现出鲁棒性能；支持障碍物避让和自运动等环境感知行为。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉形状表示从二维观测提升为可解释的三维自模型，为基于视觉的端到端控制提供了原则性替代方案，推进了连续体机器人的自主几何感知操作能力。

Abstract: Continuum robots possess high flexibility and redundancy, making them well suited for safe interaction in complex environments, yet their continuous deformation and nonlinear dynamics pose fundamental challenges to perception, modeling, and control. Existing vision-based control approaches often rely on end-to-end learning, achieving shape regulation without explicit awareness of robot geometry or its interaction with the environment. Here, we introduce a shape-interpretable visual self-modeling framework for continuum robots that enables geometry-aware control. Robot shapes are encoded from multi-view planar images using a Bezier-curve representation, transforming visual observations into a compact and physically meaningful shape space that uniquely characterizes the robot's three-dimensional configuration. Based on this representation, neural ordinary differential equations are employed to self-model both shape and end-effector dynamics directly from data, enabling hybrid shape-position control without analytical models or dense body markers. The explicit geometric structure of the learned shape space allows the robot to reason about its body and surroundings, supporting environment-aware behaviors such as obstacle avoidance and self-motion while maintaining end-effector objectives. Experiments on a cable-driven continuum robot demonstrate accurate shape-position regulation and tracking, with shape errors within 1.56% of image resolution and end-effector errors within 2% of robot length, as well as robust performance in constrained environments. By elevating visual shape representations from two-dimensional observations to an interpretable three-dimensional self-model, this work establishes a principled alternative to vision-based end-to-end control and advances autonomous, geometry-aware manipulation for continuum robots.

</details>


### [393] [Neural Implicit Action Fields: From Discrete Waypoints to Continuous Functions for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01766)
*Haoyun Liu,Jianzhuang Zhao,Xinyuan Chang,Tianle Shi,Chuanzhang Meng,Jiayuan Tan,Feng Xiong,Tong Lin,Dongjie Huo,Mu Xu,SongLin Dong,Zhiheng Ma,Yihong Gong,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经隐式动作场（NIAF），将动作预测从离散路径点转变为连续动作函数回归，实现无限分辨率的连续轨迹生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型预测离散路径点的方法与物理运动的连续性本质不匹配，离散化导致刚性采样率、缺乏高阶可微性、引入量化伪影，阻碍精确、柔顺的交互

Method: 使用MLLM作为分层频谱调制器，作用于可学习的运动先验，合成无限分辨率的连续时间流形作为轨迹；通过解析可微性实现对速度、加速度和加加速度的显式监督，确保数学一致性和物理合理性

Result: 在CALVIN和LIBERO基准测试中取得最先进结果；真实世界实验表明NIAF能够实现稳定的阻抗控制，弥合高层语义理解与低层动态执行之间的差距

Conclusion: NIAF通过连续动作函数回归范式，解决了离散路径点预测的根本局限性，实现了物理上合理、数学上一致的动作生成，为机器人控制提供了更精确、柔顺的解决方案

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, the prevailing paradigm of predicting discrete waypoints remains fundamentally misaligned with the intrinsic continuity of physical motion. This discretization imposes rigid sampling rates, lacks high-order differentiability, and introduces quantization artifacts that hinder precise, compliant interaction. We propose Neural Implicit Action Fields (NIAF), a paradigm shift that reformulates action prediction from discrete waypoints to continuous action function regression. By utilizing an MLLM as a hierarchical spectral modulator over a learnable motion prior, NIAF synthesizes infinite-resolution trajectories as continuous-time manifolds. This formulation enables analytical differentiability, allowing for explicit supervision of velocity, acceleration, and jerk to ensure mathematical consistency and physical plausibility. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on CALVIN and LIBERO benchmarks across diverse backbones. Furthermore, real-world experiments demonstrate that NIAF enables stable impedance control, bridging the gap between high-level semantic understanding and low-level dynamic execution.

</details>


### [394] [Tiny-DroNeRF: Tiny Neural Radiance Fields aboard Federated Learning-enabled Nano-drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01850)
*Ilenia Carboni,Elia Cereda,Lorenzo Lamberti,Daniele Malpetti,Francesco Conti,Daniele Palossi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tiny-DroNeRF：基于Instant-NGP的轻量化NeRF模型，专为搭载GAP9超低功耗MCU的纳米无人机设计，通过联邦学习实现分布式训练，内存占用减少96%，精度下降仅5.7dB


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机（<30g）在工业检测和搜救任务中具有独特优势，但其资源受限（<100mW MCU，<100MB内存）。现有NeRF方法需要GB级内存和GPU计算，无法在纳米无人机上运行。需要开发能在超低功耗MCU上运行的轻量化3D重建方法。

Method: 1. 基于Instant-NGP开发Tiny-DroNeRF轻量化NeRF模型；2. 针对GAP9超低功耗MCU优化；3. 采用联邦学习方案，在多架纳米无人机间分布式训练模型；4. 解决单机内存无法容纳训练数据的问题。

Result: 1. 内存占用相比Instant-NGP减少96%；2. 重建精度仅下降5.7dB；3. 联邦学习使模型能处理超出单机内存容量的数据，提升整体重建精度；4. 首次在超低功耗MCU上实现NeRF训练与纳米无人机联邦学习的结合。

Conclusion: Tiny-DroNeRF成功在资源受限的纳米无人机上实现了NeRF-based 3D重建，通过轻量化设计和联邦学习方案克服了内存和计算限制，为纳米无人机在复杂视觉任务中的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Sub-30g nano-sized aerial robots can leverage their agility and form factor to autonomously explore cluttered and narrow environments, like in industrial inspection and search and rescue missions. However, the price for their tiny size is a strong limit in their resources, i.e., sub-100 mW microcontroller units (MCUs) delivering $\sim$100 GOps/s at best, and memory budgets well below 100 MB. Despite these strict constraints, we aim to enable complex vision-based tasks aboard nano-drones, such as dense 3D scene reconstruction: a key robotic task underlying fundamental capabilities like spatial awareness and motion planning. Top-performing 3D reconstruction methods leverage neural radiance fields (NeRF) models, which require GBs of memory and massive computation, usually delivered by high-end GPUs consuming 100s of Watts. Our work introduces Tiny-DroNeRF, a lightweight NeRF model, based on Instant-NGP, and optimized for running on a GAP9 ultra-low-power (ULP) MCU aboard our nano-drones. Then, we further empower our Tiny-DroNeRF by leveraging a collaborative federated learning scheme, which distributes the model training among multiple nano-drones. Our experimental results show a 96% reduction in Tiny-DroNeRF's memory footprint compared to Instant-NGP, with only a 5.7 dB drop in reconstruction accuracy. Finally, our federated learning scheme allows Tiny-DroNeRF to train with an amount of data otherwise impossible to keep in a single drone's memory, increasing the overall reconstruction accuracy. Ultimately, our work combines, for the first time, NeRF training on an ULP MCU with federated learning on nano-drones.

</details>


### [395] [SaferPath: Hierarchical Visual Navigation with Learned Guidance and Safety-Constrained Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01898)
*Lingjie Zhang,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaferPath：一种分层视觉导航框架，利用端到端模型的学习指导，通过安全约束优化控制模块进行细化，在复杂环境中实现安全导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习导航方法在未见、杂乱或狭窄环境中存在泛化性和安全性问题，特别是在密集室内环境中容易发生碰撞。需要一种既能利用学习模型优势又能确保安全性的导航框架。

Method: 提出SaferPath分层框架：1) 将视觉观测转换为可通行区域地图；2) 使用端到端模型生成初始指导轨迹；3) 通过模型预测Stein变分进化策略(MP-SVES)进行安全约束优化，仅需几次迭代即可生成安全轨迹；4) 使用MPC控制器跟踪优化后的轨迹。

Result: 在包含未见障碍物、密集非结构化空间和狭窄走廊的场景中进行广泛实验，SaferPath持续提高成功率、减少碰撞，优于ViNT和NoMaD等基线方法，能够在具有挑战性的现实环境中实现安全导航。

Conclusion: SaferPath通过结合学习指导和基于优化的安全约束，有效解决了端到端视觉导航方法在复杂环境中的泛化和安全问题，为移动机器人在密集室内环境中的安全导航提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Visual navigation is a core capability for mobile robots, yet end-to-end learning-based methods often struggle with generalization and safety in unseen, cluttered, or narrow environments. These limitations are especially pronounced in dense indoor settings, where collisions are likely and end-to-end models frequently fail. To address this, we propose SaferPath, a hierarchical visual navigation framework that leverages learned guidance from existing end-to-end models and refines it through a safety-constrained optimization-control module. SaferPath transforms visual observations into a traversable-area map and refines guidance trajectories using Model Predictive Stein Variational Evolution Strategy (MP-SVES), efficiently generating safe trajectories in only a few iterations. The refined trajectories are tracked by an MPC controller, ensuring robust navigation in complex environments. Extensive experiments in scenarios with unseen obstacles, dense unstructured spaces, and narrow corridors demonstrate that SaferPath consistently improves success rates and reduces collisions, outperforming representative baselines such as ViNT and NoMaD, and enabling safe navigation in challenging real-world settings.

</details>


### [396] [Closed-Loop Action Chunks with Dynamic Corrections for Training-Free Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01953)
*Pengyuan Wu,Pingrui Zhang,Zhigang Wang,Dong Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DCDP是一个动态闭环扩散策略框架，通过集成基于块的动作生成与实时校正，提升动态场景下的机器人操作适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的策略在机器人操作中取得了显著成果，但在动态场景中适应能力不足，导致响应延迟或任务失败。需要一种能够实时适应环境变化的策略框架。

Method: DCDP框架包含自监督动态特征编码器、交叉注意力融合和不对称动作编码器-解码器。通过块状动作生成与实时校正，在执行前注入环境动态信息，实现闭环动作修正。

Result: 在动态PushT仿真中，DCDP在不重新训练的情况下将适应性提高了19%，仅需额外5%的计算开销。模块化设计支持即插即用集成，在动态机器人场景中实现时间一致性和实时响应性。

Conclusion: DCDP通过动态闭环校正机制有效提升了扩散策略在动态场景中的适应能力，在保持计算效率的同时显著改善了实时响应性能，适用于真实世界操作任务。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have achieved remarkable results in robotic manipulation but often struggle to adapt rapidly in dynamic scenarios, leading to delayed responses or task failures. We present DCDP, a Dynamic Closed-Loop Diffusion Policy framework that integrates chunk-based action generation with real-time correction. DCDP integrates a self-supervised dynamic feature encoder, cross-attention fusion, and an asymmetric action encoder-decoder to inject environmental dynamics before action execution, achieving real-time closed-loop action correction and enhancing the system's adaptability in dynamic scenarios. In dynamic PushT simulations, DCDP improves adaptability by 19\% without retraining while requiring only 5\% additional computation. Its modular design enables plug-and-play integration, achieving both temporal coherence and real-time responsiveness in dynamic robotic scenarios, including real-world manipulation tasks. The project page is at: https://github.com/wupengyuan/dcdp

</details>


### [397] [From Transportation to Manipulation: Transforming Magnetic Levitation to Magnetic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01982)
*Lara Bergmann,Noah Greis,Klaus Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种低成本六自由度并联运动平台6D-Platform MagBot，它将两个磁悬浮移动器耦合为复合机器人平台，实现了亚毫米定位精度和自主对接，显著扩展了工作空间、负载能力和功能灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁悬浮系统在工业自动化中主要应用于物料输送，但其在操作方面的潜力尚未充分开发。现有磁悬浮系统主要用于机内运输，缺乏将运输与操作统一的能力，限制了其在需要灵活性和适应性的制造环境中的应用。

Method: 提出6D-Platform MagBot平台，采用低成本六自由度并联运动学结构，将两个磁悬浮移动器耦合形成复合机器人平台。系统支持通过对接站实现完全自主的拾取和放置，允许快速可重复的机器重新配置。

Result: 实验表明，6D-Platform MagBot实现了亚毫米级定位精度，能够支持完全自主的拾取和放置操作。相对于单个移动器，该平台显著扩展了可达工作空间、有效载荷和功能灵活性。

Conclusion: 通过统一运输和操作功能，这项工作将磁悬浮技术推向磁悬浮机器人领域，实现了更敏捷、高效和适应性强的制造解决方案，为高混合低批量制造提供了动态吞吐量优化的新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic Levitation (MagLev) systems fundamentally increase the flexibility of in-machine material flow in industrial automation. Therefore, these systems enable dynamic throughput optimization, which is especially beneficial for high-mix low-volume manufacturing. Until now, MagLev installations have been used primarily for in-machine transport, while their potential for manipulation is largely unexplored. This paper introduces the 6D-Platform MagBot, a low-cost six degrees of freedom parallel kinematic that couples two movers into a composite robotic platform. Experiments show that the 6D-Platform MagBot achieves sub-millimeter positioning accuracy and supports fully autonomous pick up and drop off via a docking station, allowing rapid and repeatable reconfiguration of the machine. Relative to a single mover, the proposed platform substantially expands the reachable workspace, payload, and functional dexterity. By unifying transportation and manipulation, this work advances Magnetic Levitation towards Magnetic Robotics, enabling manufacturing solutions that are more agile, efficient, and adaptable.

</details>


### [398] [Learning Vision-Based Omnidirectional Navigation: A Teacher-Student Approach Using Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01999)
*Jan Finke,Wayne Paul Martis,Adrian Schmelter,Lars Erbach,Christian Jestel,Marvin Wiedemann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉的教师-学生框架用于移动机器人导航，通过深度预测替代2D LiDAR，解决3D障碍物检测问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中可靠的障碍物避障需要3D场景理解，但广泛使用的2D LiDAR传感器只能感知环境的单个水平切片，会错过扫描平面上方或下方的关键障碍物

Method: 采用教师-学生框架：教师策略使用PPO在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中训练，利用特权2D LiDAR观测（考虑完整机器人足迹）；将学习到的行为蒸馏到学生策略，该策略仅依赖由微调的Depth Anything V2模型从四个RGB摄像头预测的单目深度图

Result: 在仿真中，学生策略成功率82-96.5%，始终优于标准2D LiDAR教师（50-89%）；在真实世界实验中，基于MDE的学生在导航具有复杂3D几何形状的障碍物（如悬垂结构和低矮物体）时优于2D LiDAR教师

Conclusion: 提出的视觉导航框架成功消除了对LiDAR传感器的需求，通过单目深度估计实现了对3D障碍物的更好感知，整个推理管道可在嵌入式设备上运行，无需外部计算

Abstract: Reliable obstacle avoidance in industrial settings demands 3D scene understanding, but widely used 2D LiDAR sensors perceive only a single horizontal slice of the environment, missing critical obstacles above or below the scan plane. We present a teacher-student framework for vision-based mobile robot navigation that eliminates the need for LiDAR sensors. A teacher policy trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab leverages privileged 2D LiDAR observations that account for the full robot footprint to learn robust navigation. The learned behavior is distilled into a student policy that relies solely on monocular depth maps predicted by a fine-tuned Depth Anything V2 model from four RGB cameras. The complete inference pipeline, comprising monocular depth estimation (MDE), policy execution, and motor control, runs entirely onboard an NVIDIA Jetson Orin AGX mounted on a DJI RoboMaster platform, requiring no external computation for inference. In simulation, the student achieves success rates of 82-96.5%, consistently outperforming the standard 2D LiDAR teacher (50-89%). In real-world experiments, the MDE-based student outperforms the 2D LiDAR teacher when navigating around obstacles with complex 3D geometries, such as overhanging structures and low-profile objects, that fall outside the single scan plane of a 2D LiDAR.

</details>


### [399] [CHOP: Counterfactual Human Preference Labels Improve Obstacle Avoidance in Visuomotor Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02004)
*Gershom Seneviratne,Jianyu An,Vaibhav Shende,Sahire Ellahy,Yaxita Amin,Kondapi Manasanjani,Samarth Chopra,Jonathan Deepak Kannan,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHOP利用反事实人类偏好标签对齐视觉运动导航策略，通过人类对替代轨迹的偏好标注来提升机器人的安全导航和障碍物避让能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动导航策略在复杂现实环境中存在安全导航和动态障碍物避让的困难，需要更好地与人类的安全直觉和障碍物避让偏好对齐。

Method: CHOP方法为每个视觉观察生成一组反事实导航轨迹（机器人可能采取的替代路径），让人类标注者基于碰撞风险和路径效率等因素提供成对偏好标签，然后使用这些聚合的偏好来微调视觉运动导航策略。

Result: 在SCAND数据集上，CHOP微调的导航策略将接近碰撞事件减少49.7%，与人类偏好轨迹的偏差减少45.0%，平均障碍物间隙增加19.8%。在Ghost Robotics Vision60四足机器人上的实际部署中，目标成功率提高24.4%，最小障碍物间隙增加6.8%，碰撞和干预事件减少45.7%，归一化路径完成度提高38.6%。

Conclusion: 反事实偏好监督在弥合大规模视觉运动策略与人类对齐、安全感知的具身导航之间的差距方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Visuomotor navigation policies have shown strong perception-action coupling for embodied agents, yet they often struggle with safe navigation and dynamic obstacle avoidance in complex real-world environments. We introduce CHOP, a novel approach that leverages Counterfactual Human Preference Labels to align visuomotor navigation policies towards human intuition of safety and obstacle avoidance in navigation. In CHOP, for each visual observation, the robot's executed trajectory is included among a set of counterfactual navigation trajectories: alternative trajectories the robot could have followed under identical conditions. Human annotators provide pairwise preference labels over these trajectories based on anticipated outcomes such as collision risk and path efficiency. These aggregated preferences are then used to fine-tune visuomotor navigation policies, aligning their behavior with human preferences in navigation. Experiments on the SCAND dataset show that visuomotor navigation policies fine-tuned with CHOP reduce near-collision events by 49.7%, decrease deviation from human-preferred trajectories by 45.0%, and increase average obstacle clearance by 19.8% on average across multiple state-of-the-art models, compared to their pretrained baselines. These improvements transfer to real-world deployments on a Ghost Robotics Vision60 quadruped, where CHOP-aligned policies improve average goal success rates by 24.4%, increase minimum obstacle clearance by 6.8%, reduce collision and intervention events by 45.7%, and improve normalized path completion by 38.6% on average across navigation scenarios, compared to their pretrained baselines. Our results highlight the value of counterfactual preference supervision in bridging the gap between large-scale visuomotor policies and human-aligned, safety-aware embodied navigation.

</details>


### [400] [LAD-Drive: Bridging Language and Trajectory with Action-Aware Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02035)
*Fabian Schmidt,Karol Fedurko,Markus Enzweiler,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAD-Drive是一个生成式自动驾驶框架，通过结构化解耦高层意图和低层空间规划，使用概率元动作分布和动作感知扩散解码器将MLLM的语义知识转化为连续轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1）依赖单模态规划头，限制了多模态驾驶行为的表示能力；2）大多数生成方法使用one-hot编码的动作，忽略了复杂场景中关键的导航不确定性。需要将MLLM的离散语义知识转化为连续轨迹。

Method: LAD-Drive采用结构化解耦方法：1）动作解码器推断概率元动作分布，建立显式信念状态；2）该分布与车辆运动状态融合，条件化动作感知扩散解码器；3）使用截断去噪过程将学习的运动锚点细化为安全、运动学可行的轨迹。

Result: 在LangAuto基准测试中，LAD-Drive实现了最先进的结果，比竞争基线在驾驶分数上提升了高达59%，同时显著减少了路线偏差和碰撞。

Conclusion: LAD-Drive通过结构化解耦高层意图和低层规划，有效解决了将MLLM语义知识转化为连续轨迹的挑战，在自动驾驶任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide advanced reasoning for autonomous driving, translating their discrete semantic knowledge into continuous trajectories remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods often rely on unimodal planning heads that inherently limit their ability to represent multimodal driving behavior. Furthermore, most generative approaches frequently condition on one-hot encoded actions, discarding the nuanced navigational uncertainty critical for complex scenarios. To resolve these limitations, we introduce LAD-Drive, a generative framework that structurally disentangles high-level intention from low-level spatial planning. LAD-Drive employs an action decoder to infer a probabilistic meta-action distribution, establishing an explicit belief state that preserves the nuanced intent typically lost by one-hot encodings. This distribution, fused with the vehicle's kinematic state, conditions an action-aware diffusion decoder that utilizes a truncated denoising process to refine learned motion anchors into safe, kinematically feasible trajectories. Extensive evaluations on the LangAuto benchmark demonstrate that LAD-Drive achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming competitive baselines by up to 59% in Driving Score while significantly reducing route deviations and collisions. We will publicly release the code and models on https://github.com/iis-esslingen/lad-drive.

</details>


### [401] [ACDC: Adaptive Curriculum Planning with Dynamic Contrastive Control for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02104)
*Xuerui Wang,Guangyu Ren,Tianhong Dai,Bintao Hu,Shuangyao Huang,Wenzhang Zhang,Hengyan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACDC：一种结合自适应课程规划与动态对比控制的机器人操作强化学习新范式，通过多维课程调度和对比学习实现更高效的学习轨迹


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标条件强化学习方法依赖经验优先级，导致跨任务性能受限。受人类学习行为启发，需要更全面的学习范式来引导智能体沿着精心设计的学习轨迹前进

Method: ACDC框架包含两个层面：规划层面的自适应课程（AC）组件，基于智能体成功率和训练进度动态平衡多样性驱动探索与质量驱动利用；控制层面的动态对比（DC）组件，通过范数约束对比学习实现与当前课程重点对齐的幅度引导经验选择

Result: 在具有挑战性的机器人操作任务上的大量实验表明，ACDC在样本效率和最终任务成功率方面始终优于最先进的基线方法

Conclusion: ACDC通过整合多维自适应课程规划与动态对比控制，为机器人操作提供了一种更有效的学习范式，显著提升了强化学习的性能表现

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning has shown considerable potential in robotic manipulation; however, existing approaches remain limited by their reliance on prioritizing collected experience, resulting in suboptimal performance across diverse tasks. Inspired by human learning behaviors, we propose a more comprehensive learning paradigm, ACDC, which integrates multidimensional Adaptive Curriculum (AC) Planning with Dynamic Contrastive (DC) Control to guide the agent along a well-designed learning trajectory. More specifically, at the planning level, the AC component schedules the learning curriculum by dynamically balancing diversity-driven exploration and quality-driven exploitation based on the agent's success rate and training progress. At the control level, the DC component implements the curriculum plan through norm-constrained contrastive learning, enabling magnitude-guided experience selection aligned with the current curriculum focus. Extensive experiments on challenging robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ACDC consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines in both sample efficiency and final task success rate.

</details>


### [402] [Robometer: Scaling General-Purpose Robotic Reward Models via Trajectory Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02115)
*Anthony Liang,Yigit Korkmaz,Jiahui Zhang,Minyoung Hwang,Abrar Anwar,Sidhant Kaushik,Aditya Shah,Alex S. Huang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Dieter Fox,Yu Xiang,Anqi Li,Andreea Bobu,Abhishek Gupta,Stephen Tu,Erdem Biyik,Jesse Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robometer是一个可扩展的机器人奖励建模框架，结合轨迹内进度监督和轨迹间偏好监督，使用双重目标训练，在包含失败和次优轨迹的大规模数据集上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用机器人奖励模型通常基于专家演示预测绝对任务进度，仅提供局部、帧级监督。这种方法在专家演示上有效，但在大规模机器人数据集中扩展性差，因为失败和次优轨迹丰富，且分配密集进度标签存在模糊性。

Method: Robometer采用双重目标训练：1) 帧级进度损失，在专家数据上锚定奖励幅度；2) 轨迹比较偏好损失，对相同任务的轨迹施加全局排序约束。该方法支持从真实和增强的失败轨迹中有效学习。为支持大规模训练，作者构建了RBM-1M数据集，包含超过100万条轨迹，涵盖多样机器人本体和任务。

Result: 在基准测试和真实世界评估中，Robometer学习到的奖励函数比先前方法更具泛化性，并在多样下游应用中提高了机器人学习性能。

Conclusion: Robometer通过结合轨迹内进度监督和轨迹间偏好监督，解决了大规模机器人数据集中奖励建模的扩展性问题，学习到更泛化的奖励函数，显著提升了机器人学习性能。

Abstract: General-purpose robot reward models are typically trained to predict absolute task progress from expert demonstrations, providing only local, frame-level supervision. While effective for expert demonstrations, this paradigm scales poorly to large-scale robotics datasets where failed and suboptimal trajectories are abundant and assigning dense progress labels is ambiguous. We introduce Robometer, a scalable reward modeling framework that combines intra-trajectory progress supervision with inter-trajectory preference supervision. Robometer is trained with a dual objective: a frame-level progress loss that anchors reward magnitude on expert data, and a trajectory-comparison preference loss that imposes global ordering constraints across trajectories of the same task, enabling effective learning from both real and augmented failed trajectories. To support this formulation at scale, we curate RBM-1M, a reward-learning dataset comprising over one million trajectories spanning diverse robot embodiments and tasks, including substantial suboptimal and failure data. Across benchmarks and real-world evaluations, Robometer learns more generalizable reward functions than prior methods and improves robot learning performance across a diverse set of downstream applications. Code, model weights, and videos at https://robometer.github.io/.

</details>


### [403] [Rethinking Camera Choice: An Empirical Study on Fisheye Camera Properties in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02139)
*Han Xue,Nan Min,Xiaotong Liu,Wendi Chen,Yuan Fang,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次对腕戴式鱼眼相机在模仿学习中的影响进行系统性实证研究，发现宽视场角在复杂环境中显著提升空间定位能力，但需要足够的环境多样性来避免过拟合，并通过随机尺度增强策略改善硬件泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼眼相机在机器人操作中因其超宽视场角而被广泛采用，但其对策略学习的具体影响缺乏系统性理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，为鱼眼数据集的大规模收集和有效使用提供实证依据。

Method: 通过仿真和真实世界的大量实验，研究三个关键问题：空间定位、场景泛化和硬件泛化。采用随机尺度增强策略来改善跨相机迁移性能，分析不同环境复杂度下的训练效果。

Result: 1) 宽视场角在复杂环境中显著增强空间定位能力；2) 鱼眼训练的策略在简单场景中容易过拟合，但在足够环境多样性下展现出优越的场景泛化能力；3) 发现跨相机迁移失败的根本原因是尺度过拟合，随机尺度增强策略能有效提升硬件泛化性能。

Conclusion: 研究为机器人学习中鱼眼数据集的大规模收集和有效使用提供了具体可行的指导，揭示了鱼眼相机在模仿学习中的关键特性和优化策略。

Abstract: The adoption of fisheye cameras in robotic manipulation, driven by their exceptionally wide Field of View (FoV), is rapidly outpacing a systematic understanding of their downstream effects on policy learning. This paper presents the first comprehensive empirical study to bridge this gap, rigorously analyzing the properties of wrist-mounted fisheye cameras for imitation learning. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world, we investigate three critical research questions: spatial localization, scene generalization, and hardware generalization. Our investigation reveals that: (1) The wide FoV significantly enhances spatial localization, but this benefit is critically contingent on the visual complexity of the environment. (2) Fisheye-trained policies, while prone to overfitting in simple scenes, unlock superior scene generalization when trained with sufficient environmental diversity. (3) While naive cross-camera transfer leads to failures, we identify the root cause as scale overfitting and demonstrate that hardware generalization performance can be improved with a simple Random Scale Augmentation (RSA) strategy. Collectively, our findings provide concrete, actionable guidance for the large-scale collection and effective use of fisheye datasets in robotic learning. More results and videos are available on https://robo-fisheye.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [404] [Multi-Sourced, Multi-Agent Evidence Retrieval for Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00267)
*Shuzhi Gong,Richard O. Sinnott,Jianzhong Qi,Cecile Paris,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WKGFC利用权威开放知识图谱作为核心证据源，通过LLM驱动的检索获取相关子图作为结构化证据，并补充网页内容，以马尔可夫决策过程自动执行，优化提示以适配事实核查任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：基于语义和社会上下文模式的方法泛化能力有限；基于检索增强生成的方法主要依赖文本相似性检索，难以捕捉多跳语义关系，导致证据检索时忽略细微事实关联，影响验证准确性。

Method: 1. 利用权威开放知识图谱作为核心证据源；2. LLM驱动的检索评估声明并检索最相关的知识子图，形成结构化证据；3. 检索网页内容补充知识图谱证据；4. 将上述过程实现为自动马尔可夫决策过程：推理LLM智能体根据当前证据和声明决定行动；5. 通过提示优化微调智能体LLM以适配事实核查任务。

Result: 论文未在摘要中提供具体实验结果，但提出的方法旨在解决现有方法的局限性，通过结构化知识图谱证据和多跳语义关系捕捉来提高事实核查的准确性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: WKGFC通过整合权威知识图谱和LLM驱动的检索，结合MDP框架和提示优化，能够更好地捕捉多跳语义关系，提供更准确的事实核查证据检索和验证能力。

Abstract: Misinformation spreading over the Internet poses a significant threat to both societies and individuals, necessitating robust and scalable fact-checking that relies on retrieving accurate and trustworthy evidence. Previous methods rely on semantic and social-contextual patterns learned from training data, which limits their generalization to new data distributions. Recently, Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) based methods have been proposed to utilize the reasoning capability of LLMs with retrieved grounding evidence documents. However, these methods largely rely on textual similarity for evidence retrieval and struggle to retrieve evidence that captures multi-hop semantic relations within rich document contents. These limitations lead to overlooking subtle factual correlations between the evidence and the claims to be fact-checked during evidence retrieval, thus causing inaccurate veracity predictions.
  To address these issues, we propose WKGFC, which exploits authorized open knowledge graph as a core resource of evidence. LLM-enabled retrieval is designed to assess the claims and retrieve the most relevant knowledge subgraphs, forming structured evidence for fact verification. To augment the knowledge graph evidence, we retrieve web contents for completion. The above process is implemented as an automatic Markov Decision Process (MDP): A reasoning LLM agent decides what actions to take according to the current evidence and the claims. To adapt the MDP for fact-checking, we use prompt optimization to fine-tune the agentic LLM.

</details>


### [405] [EmCoop: A Framework and Benchmark for Embodied Cooperation Among LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00349)
*Hanqing Yang,Shiyu Chen,Narjes Nourzad,Marie Siew,Jingdi Chen,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmCoop：一个用于研究基于LLM的具身多智能体系统中合作行为的基准框架，通过分离认知层和具身交互层来分析合作动态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景需要多个具身智能体在动态环境中协作完成任务，但现有基准难以对合作如何产生、展开和促进任务成功进行细粒度分析

Method: 提出EmCoop框架，分离高层认知层和低层具身交互层，通过时间上的交错动态来表征智能体合作；提出通用的过程级指标来诊断合作质量和失败模式；在两个具身环境中实例化该框架

Result: EmCoop框架能够扩展到任意数量的智能体并支持多样化的通信拓扑结构，使研究人员能够系统分析不同团队规模和任务设置下的合作动态

Conclusion: EmCoop为研究基于LLM的具身多智能体系统中的合作行为提供了一个系统化的分析框架，超越了最终任务成功的评估，能够深入分析合作过程动态

Abstract: Real-world scenarios increasingly require multiple embodied agents to collaborate in dynamic environments under embodied constraints, as many tasks exceed the capabilities of any single agent. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable high-level cognitive coordination through reasoning, planning, and natural language communication. However, fine-grained analyses of how such collaboration emerges, unfolds, and contributes to task success in embodied multi-agent systems are difficult to conduct with existing benchmarks. In this paper, we introduce EmCoop, a benchmark framework for studying cooperation in LLM-based embodied multi-agent systems. Our framework separates a high-level cognitive layer from a low-level embodied interaction layer, allowing us to characterize agent cooperation through their interleaved dynamics over time. Given a cooperation-constrained embodied task, we propose generalizable, process-level metrics that diagnose collaboration quality and failure modes, beyond final task success. We instantiate our framework in two embodied environments that scale to arbitrary numbers of agents and support diverse communication topologies, and use these instantiations to demonstrate how EmCoop enables systematic analysis of cooperation dynamics across team sizes and task settings. The project web page can be found at: https://happyeureka.github.io/emcoop.

</details>


### [406] [Monotropic Artificial Intelligence: Toward a Cognitive Taxonomy of Domain-Specialized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00350)
*Antonio de Sousa Leitão Filho,Allan Kardec Duailibe Barros Filho,Fabrício Saul Lima,Selby Mykael Lima dos Santos,Rejani Bandeira Vieira Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"单焦点人工智能"概念，主张通过牺牲通用性在狭窄领域实现极致精确，挑战了AI研究追求通用性的主流范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究范式将进步等同于规模扩大，认为更大模型在更广数据集上训练就能获得更强能力。这种假设虽然对通用应用有效，但忽视了知识的广度与深度之间的根本认识论张力。作者旨在挑战人工智能通用性作为唯一合法目标的隐含假设。

Method: 引入"单焦点人工智能"概念，借鉴理解自闭症认知的单焦点理论，形式化单焦点模型的特征特征，并与传统多焦点架构对比。通过Mini-Enedina模型（3750万参数）进行验证，该模型在Timoshenko梁分析上实现近乎完美性能，但在其领域外保持故意无能。

Result: 证明了单焦点模型的可行性：Mini-Enedina在狭窄的Timoshenko梁分析领域实现了近乎完美的性能，同时在其专业领域外保持故意无能。这表明牺牲通用性可以在特定领域获得卓越精确性。

Conclusion: 挑战了人工通用智能是AI研究唯一合法目标的隐含假设，提出了一种认知生态学框架，其中专门化和通用系统可以互补共存。单焦点AI代表了安全关键应用的替代认知架构，具有独特优势。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm in artificial intelligence research equates progress with scale: larger models trained on broader datasets are presumed to yield superior capabilities. This assumption, while empirically productive for general-purpose applications, obscures a fundamental epistemological tension between breadth and depth of knowledge. We introduce the concept of \emph{Monotropic Artificial Intelligence} -- language models that deliberately sacrifice generality to achieve extraordinary precision within narrowly circumscribed domains. Drawing on the cognitive theory of monotropism developed to understand autistic cognition, we argue that intense specialization represents not a limitation but an alternative cognitive architecture with distinct advantages for safety-critical applications. We formalize the defining characteristics of monotropic models, contrast them with conventional polytropic architectures, and demonstrate their viability through Mini-Enedina, a 37.5-million-parameter model that achieves near-perfect performance on Timoshenko beam analysis while remaining deliberately incompetent outside its domain. Our framework challenges the implicit assumption that artificial general intelligence constitutes the sole legitimate aspiration of AI research, proposing instead a cognitive ecology in which specialized and generalist systems coexist complementarily.

</details>


### [407] [Conservative Equilibrium Discovery in Offline Game-Theoretic Multiagent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00374)
*Austin A. Nguyen,Michael P. Wellman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COffeE-PSRO：一种用于离线多智能体博弈求解的新方法，通过结合保守主义原则和不确定性量化，在固定轨迹数据集上寻找低后悔的均衡策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线学习在多智能体混合动机博弈中面临挑战：固定数据集仅能提供有限博弈动态信息，难以验证候选均衡的真实性，且无法保证找到的解决方案在真实博弈中具有低后悔。

Method: 扩展PSRO（策略空间响应预言机）框架，量化博弈动态不确定性，修改强化学习目标以偏向在真实博弈中更可能具有低后悔的解决方案。提出专门为离线设置设计的新型元策略求解器，指导PSRO中的策略探索。结合离线强化学习的保守主义原则，形成COffeE-PSRO方法。

Result: 实验表明COffeE-PSRO能够提取比现有最先进离线方法更低后悔的解决方案，并揭示了算法组件、经验博弈保真度和整体性能之间的关系。

Conclusion: COffeE-PSRO成功将保守主义原则整合到离线博弈求解中，通过量化不确定性和修改学习目标，在有限数据集条件下有效寻找低后悔均衡策略，为离线多智能体学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Offline learning of strategies takes data efficiency to its extreme by restricting algorithms to a fixed dataset of state-action trajectories. We consider the problem in a mixed-motive multiagent setting, where the goal is to solve a game under the offline learning constraint. We first frame this problem in terms of selecting among candidate equilibria. Since datasets may inform only a small fraction of game dynamics, it is generally infeasible in offline game-solving to even verify a proposed solution is a true equilibrium. Therefore, we consider the relative probability of low regret (i.e., closeness to equilibrium) across candidates based on the information available. Specifically, we extend Policy Space Response Oracles (PSRO), an online game-solving approach, by quantifying game dynamics uncertainty and modifying the RL objective to skew towards solutions more likely to have low regret in the true game. We further propose a novel meta-strategy solver, tailored for the offline setting, to guide strategy exploration in PSRO. Our incorporation of Conservatism principles from Offline reinforcement learning approaches for strategy Exploration gives our approach its name: COffeE-PSRO. Experiments demonstrate COffeE-PSRO's ability to extract lower-regret solutions than state-of-the-art offline approaches and reveal relationships between algorithmic components empirical game fidelity, and overall performance.

</details>


### [408] [Confusion-Aware Rubric Optimization for LLM-based Automated Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00451)
*Yucheng Chu,Hang Li,Kaiqi Yang,Yasemin Copur-Gencturk,Joseph Krajcik,Namsoo Shin,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARO框架通过混淆矩阵分解错误信号，针对特定误分类模式生成修复补丁，避免规则稀释，提升LLM评分器的准确性和效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评分器提示优化方法存在"规则稀释"问题，将独立错误样本聚合到单一更新步骤中，导致冲突约束削弱评分逻辑，需要更精确的错误信号分离方法

Method: 提出混淆感知评分标准优化(CARO)框架：1)利用混淆矩阵将整体错误信号分解为不同误分类模式；2)针对主导错误模式生成定向"修复补丁"；3)采用多样性感知选择机制防止指导冲突；4)避免资源密集的嵌套细化循环

Result: 在教师教育和STEM数据集上的实证评估表明，CARO显著优于现有SOTA方法，证明通过模式特定的外科式修复替代混合错误聚合能提升自动评估的可扩展性和精确性

Conclusion: CARO框架通过结构化分离错误信号，解决了规则稀释问题，为LLM评分器提供了更准确、高效的提示优化方法，推动了自动评估系统的发展

Abstract: Accurate and unambiguous guidelines are critical for large language model (LLM) based graders, yet manually crafting these prompts is often sub-optimal as LLMs can misinterpret expert guidelines or lack necessary domain specificity. Consequently, the field has moved toward automated prompt optimization to refine grading guidelines without the burden of manual trial and error. However, existing frameworks typically aggregate independent and unstructured error samples into a single update step, resulting in "rule dilution" where conflicting constraints weaken the model's grading logic. To address these limitations, we introduce Confusion-Aware Rubric Optimization (CARO), a novel framework that enhances accuracy and computational efficiency by structurally separating error signals. CARO leverages the confusion matrix to decompose monolithic error signals into distinct modes, allowing for the diagnosis and repair of specific misclassification patterns individually. By synthesizing targeted "fixing patches" for dominant error modes and employing a diversity-aware selection mechanism, the framework prevents guidance conflict and eliminates the need for resource-heavy nested refinement loops. Empirical evaluations on teacher education and STEM datasets demonstrate that CARO significantly outperforms existing SOTA methods. These results suggest that replacing mixed-error aggregation with surgical, mode-specific repair yields robust improvements in automated assessment scalability and precision.

</details>


### [409] [MED-COPILOT: A Medical Assistant Powered by GraphRAG and Similar Patient Case Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00460)
*Shuheng Chen,Namratha Patil,Haonan Pan,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang,Yao Du,Ruishan Liu,Jieyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MED-COPILOT是一个结合指南知识图谱和相似患者检索的临床决策支持系统，通过GraphRAG和混合检索提升LLM的临床推理准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策需要整合异质证据，包括患者病史、临床指南和相似病例轨迹。虽然大语言模型具备推理能力，但容易产生幻觉且难以整合长结构化医疗文档。

Method: 结合指南驱动的GraphRAG检索与混合语义-关键词相似患者检索。从WHO和NICE指南构建结构化知识图谱，应用社区级摘要进行高效检索，维护包含36,000个病例的相似患者数据库（来自SOAP标准化的MIMIC-IV记录和Synthea生成数据）。

Result: 在临床笔记完成和医学问答任务上持续优于参数化LLM基线和标准RAG，提高了生成保真度和临床推理准确性。系统提供证据检查、令牌级相似度贡献可视化和引导性后续分析功能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用且可解释的方法，将结构化指南知识与患者级类比证据整合到临床LLM中，支持透明和基于证据的临床推理。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making requires synthesizing heterogeneous evidence, including patient histories, clinical guidelines, and trajectories of comparable cases. While large language models (LLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, they remain prone to hallucinations and struggle to integrate long, structured medical documents. We present MED-COPILOT, an interactive clinical decision-support system designed for clinicians and medical trainees, which combines guideline-grounded GraphRAG retrieval with hybrid semantic-keyword similar-patient retrieval to support transparent and evidence-aware clinical reasoning. The system builds a structured knowledge graph from WHO and NICE guidelines, applies community-level summarization for efficient retrieval, and maintains a 36,000-case similar-patient database derived from SOAP-normalized MIMIC-IV notes and Synthea-generated records.
  We evaluate our framework on clinical note completion and medical question answering, and demonstrate that it consistently outperforms parametric LLM baselines and standard RAG, improving both generation fidelity and clinical reasoning accuracy. The full system is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/Cryo3978/Med_GraphRAG , enabling users to inspect retrieved evidence, visualize token-level similarity contributions, and conduct guided follow-up analysis. Our results demonstrate a practical and interpretable approach to integrating structured guideline knowledge with patient-level analogical evidence for clinical LLMs.

</details>


### [410] [Optimizing In-Context Demonstrations for LLM-based Automated Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00465)
*Yucheng Chu,Hang Li,Kaiqi Yang,Yasemin Copur-Gencturk,Kevin Haudek,Joseph Krajcik,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUIDE框架通过边界聚焦优化解决自动评分中的示例选择和精炼问题，显著提升LLM在开放式回答评分中的可靠性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的自动评分系统存在两个主要问题：1）传统基于语义相似性的示例检索方法难以捕捉评分标准中的微妙决策边界；2）手动构建专家推理过程耗时且成为系统扩展的瓶颈。这些问题限制了自动评分系统在个性化教育反馈中的可靠性和可扩展性。

Method: GUIDE框架将示例选择和精炼重新定义为边界聚焦优化问题。采用迭代循环的选择和精炼过程，使用新颖的对比操作符识别"边界对"（语义相似但分数不同的回答）。通过生成区分性推理来增强示例，这些推理明确解释为什么某个回答获得特定分数而非相邻分数。

Result: 在物理、化学和教学内容知识等多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，GUIDE显著优于标准检索基线方法。该方法在边界案例上表现出特别稳健的改进，并提高了评分标准的一致性。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过聚焦模型注意力于评分标准的精确边界，为可信赖、可扩展的评估系统铺平了道路，使其更紧密地符合人类教学标准。

Abstract: Automated assessment of open-ended student responses is a critical capability for scaling personalized feedback in education. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in grading tasks via in-context learning (ICL), their reliability is heavily dependent on the selection of few-shot exemplars and the construction of high-quality rationales. Standard retrieval methods typically select examples based on semantic similarity, which often fails to capture subtle decision boundaries required for rubric adherence. Furthermore, manually crafting the expert rationales needed to guide these models can be a significant bottleneck. To address these limitations, we introduce GUIDE (Grading Using Iteratively Designed Exemplars), a framework that reframes exemplar selection and refinement in automated grading as a boundary-focused optimization problem. GUIDE operates on a continuous loop of selection and refinement, employing novel contrastive operators to identify "boundary pairs" that are semantically similar but possess different grades. We enhance exemplars by generating discriminative rationales that explicitly articulate why a response receives a specific score to the exclusion of adjacent grades. Extensive experiments across datasets in physics, chemistry, and pedagogical content knowledge demonstrate that GUIDE significantly outperforms standard retrieval baselines. By focusing the model's attention on the precise edges of rubric, our approach shows exceptionally robust gains on borderline cases and improved rubric adherence. GUIDE paves the way for trusted, scalable assessment systems that align closely with human pedagogical standards.

</details>


### [411] [Why Not? Solver-Grounded Certificates for Explainable Mission Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00469)
*Najeeb Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于优化模型证书的解释方法，为地球观测卫星调度决策提供因果可信的解释，相比后处理方法具有更好的忠实性和稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星运营商需要调度决策的合理解释（为何接受/拒绝请求，如何修改可调度），现有后处理解释方法存在非因果归因、约束组合遗漏和求解器路径依赖等问题

Method: 采用忠实性优先方法，直接从优化模型生成证书：拒绝请求使用最小不可行子集，接受请求使用紧约束和对比权衡，假设查询使用逆求解

Result: 在结构不同的约束交互调度实例中，证书方法相对于求解器约束模型实现完美忠实性（15/15约束检查），反事实有效性（7/7），稳定性（Jaccard=1.0），而后处理基线在29%情况下产生非因果归因，在所有多原因拒绝中遗漏约束组合

Conclusion: 基于优化模型证书的解释方法为卫星调度决策提供了忠实、稳定且可扩展的解释框架，优于后处理基线，支持实际运营批次的解释生成

Abstract: Operators of Earth observation satellites need justifications for scheduling decisions: why a request was selected, rejected, or what changes would make it schedulable. Existing approaches construct post-hoc reasoning layers independent of the optimizer, risking non-causal attributions, incomplete constraint conjunctions, and solver-path dependence. We take a faithfulness-first approach: every explanation is a certificate derived from the optimization model itself: minimal infeasible subsets for rejections, tight constraints and contrastive trade-offs for selections, and inverse solves for what-if queries. On a scheduling instance with structurally distinct constraint interactions, certificates achieve perfect soundness with respect to the solver's constraint model (15/15 cited-constraint checks), counterfactual validity (7/7), and stability (Jaccard = 1.0 across 28 seed-pairs), while a post-hoc baseline produces non-causal attributions in 29% of cases and misses constraint conjunctions in every multi-cause rejection. A scalability analysis up to 200 orders and 30 satellites confirms practical extraction times for operational batches.

</details>


### [412] [LifeEval: A Multimodal Benchmark for Assistive AI in Egocentric Daily Life Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00490)
*Hengjian Gao,Kaiwei Zhang,Shibo Wang,Mingjie Chen,Qihang Cao,Xianfeng Wang,Yucheng Zhu,Xiongkuo Min,Wei Sun,Dandan Zhu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifeEval是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在日常生活场景中实时人机协作能力的基准测试，强调任务导向、第一人称实时感知和自然对话交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准测试主要评估被动理解和回顾性分析，无法捕捉实时、交互式的人机协作需求。多模态大语言模型在动态现实环境中的实时辅助能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 通过严谨的标注流程构建LifeEval基准，包含4,075个高质量问答对，涵盖6个核心能力维度，强调任务导向的全面评估、第一人称连续流实时感知和自然对话交互。

Result: 对26个最先进的多模态大语言模型进行评估，结果显示在实现及时、有效和自适应交互方面存在显著挑战，突显了推进以人为中心的交互智能的必要方向。

Conclusion: LifeEval基准揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在实时人机协作方面的局限性，为开发更有效、自适应的人类辅助系统提供了重要评估框架和研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid progress of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) marks a significant step toward artificial general intelligence, offering great potential for augmenting human capabilities. However, their ability to provide effective assistance in dynamic, real-world environments remains largely underexplored. Existing video benchmarks predominantly assess passive understanding through retrospective analysis or isolated perception tasks, failing to capture the interactive and adaptive nature of real-time user assistance. To bridge this gap, we introduce LifeEval, a multimodal benchmark designed to evaluate real-time, task-oriented human-AI collaboration in daily life from an egocentric perspective. LifeEval emphasizes three key aspects: task-oriented holistic evaluation, egocentric real-time perception from continuous first-person streams, and human-assistant collaborative interaction through natural dialogues. Constructed via a rigorous annotation pipeline, the benchmark comprises 4,075 high-quality question-answer pairs across 6 core capability dimensions. Extensive evaluations of 26 state-of-the-art MLLMs on LifeEval reveal substantial challenges in achieving timely, effective and adaptive interaction, highlighting essential directions for advancing human-centered interactive intelligence.

</details>


### [413] [AI Runtime Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00495)
*Christopher Cruz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AI运行时基础设施作为模型与应用之间的执行时层，主动观察、推理和干预智能体行为以优化任务执行效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级优化和被动日志系统无法在智能体运行时进行主动干预和优化，需要一个新的执行时层来提升智能体工作流的性能、效率和可靠性

Method: 构建位于模型之上、应用之下的运行时基础设施层，该层能够主动观察智能体行为，进行推理分析，并在运行时进行干预，实现自适应内存管理、故障检测与恢复、策略执行等功能

Result: 该基础设施将执行过程本身作为优化对象，能够优化任务成功率、延迟、令牌效率、可靠性和安全性，特别适用于长期视野的智能体工作流

Conclusion: AI运行时基础设施代表了一种新的优化范式，将执行时干预作为提升智能体性能的关键手段，为复杂智能体系统的可靠运行提供了系统级支持

Abstract: We introduce AI Runtime Infrastructure, a distinct execution-time layer that operates above the model and below the application, actively observing, reasoning over, and intervening in agent behavior to optimize task success, latency, token efficiency, reliability, and safety while the agent is running. Unlike model-level optimizations or passive logging systems, runtime infrastructure treats execution itself as an optimization surface, enabling adaptive memory management, failure detection, recovery, and policy enforcement over long-horizon agent workflows.

</details>


### [414] [Advancing Multimodal Judge Models through a Capability-Oriented Benchmark and MCTS-Driven Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00546)
*Zeyu Chen,Huanjin Yao,Ziwang Zhao,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: M-JudgeBench：一个十维能力导向的基准测试，用于全面评估多模态大语言模型作为评判者的能力，通过分解为成对思维链比较、长度偏差避免和过程错误检测等任务来诊断模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评判基准按任务类型分类样本，但未能捕捉可靠评估所需的基本判断能力。需要建立更原则性的基础来评估MLLM作为评判者的能力和可靠性，以确保可信评估。

Method: 1. 提出M-JudgeBench十维能力导向基准，将评估分解为成对思维链比较、长度偏差避免和过程错误检测任务，涵盖十个细粒度子任务；2. 提出Judge-MCTS数据构建框架，生成具有不同正确性和长度的成对推理轨迹；3. 使用Judge-MCTS构建MCTS增强数据集并训练M-Judger系列强评判模型。

Result: 系统评估揭示了现有MLLM-as-a-judge系统的系统性弱点。M-Judger在现有评判基准和M-JudgeBench上均表现出优越性能，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过M-JudgeBench和Judge-MCTS框架为评估MLLM-as-a-judge建立了更原则性的基础，为未来评判模型评估和能力驱动的评判训练研究铺平了道路。
